use std::{
    borrow::{Borrow, Cow},
    fmt,
    iter::{self, FusedIterator},
    mem::{self, ManuallyDrop},
    ops, ptr, slice,
};

use countme::Count;

use crate::{
    arc::{Arc, HeaderSlice, ThinArc},
    green::{GreenElement, GreenElementRef, SyntaxKind},
    utility_types::static_assert,
    GreenToken, NodeOrToken, TextRange, TextSize,
};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub(super) struct GreenNodeHead {
    kind: SyntaxKind,
    text_len: TextSize,
    _c: Count<GreenNode>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub(crate) enum GreenChild {
    Node { rel_offset: TextSize, node: GreenNode },
    Token { rel_offset: TextSize, token: GreenToken },
}
#[cfg(target_pointer_width = "64")]
static_assert!(mem::size_of::<GreenChild>() == mem::size_of::<usize>() * 2);

type Repr = HeaderSlice<GreenNodeHead, [GreenChild]>;
type ReprThin = HeaderSlice<GreenNodeHead, [GreenChild; 0]>;
#[repr(transparent)]
pub struct GreenNodeData {
    data: ReprThin,
}

impl PartialEq for GreenNodeData {
    fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {
        self.header() == other.header() && self.slice() == other.slice()
    }
}

/// Internal node in the immutable tree.
/// It has other nodes and tokens as children.
#[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
#[repr(transparent)]
pub struct GreenNode {
    ptr: ThinArc<GreenNodeHead, GreenChild>,
}

impl ToOwned for GreenNodeData {
    type Owned = GreenNode;

    #[inline]
    fn to_owned(&self) -> GreenNode {
        unsafe {
            let green = GreenNode::from_raw(ptr::NonNull::from(self));
            let green = ManuallyDrop::new(green);
            GreenNode::clone(&green)
        }
    }
}

impl Borrow<GreenNodeData> for GreenNode {
    #[inline]
    fn borrow(&self) -> &GreenNodeData {
        &*self
    }
}

impl From<Cow<'_, GreenNodeData>> for GreenNode {
    #[inline]
    fn from(cow: Cow<'_, GreenNodeData>) -> Self {
        cow.into_owned()
    }
}

impl fmt::Debug for GreenNodeData {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("GreenNode")
            .field("kind", &self.kind())
            .field("text_len", &self.text_len())
            .field("n_children", &self.children().len())
            .finish()
    }
}

impl fmt::Debug for GreenNode {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        let data: &GreenNodeData = &*self;
        fmt::Debug::fmt(data, f)
    }
}

impl fmt::Display for GreenNode {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        let data: &GreenNodeData = &*self;
        fmt::Display::fmt(data, f)
    }
}

impl fmt::Display for GreenNodeData {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        for child in self.children() {
            write!(f, "{}", child)?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl GreenNodeData {
    #[inline]
    fn header(&self) -> &GreenNodeHead {
        &self.data.header
    }

    #[inline]
    fn slice(&self) -> &[GreenChild] {
        self.data.slice()
    }

    /// Kind of this node.
    #[inline]
    pub fn kind(&self) -> SyntaxKind {
        self.header().kind
    }

    /// Returns the length of the text covered by this node.
    #[inline]
    pub fn text_len(&self) -> TextSize {
        self.header().text_len
    }

    /// Children of this node.
    #[inline]
    pub fn children(&self) -> Children<'_> {
        Children { raw: self.slice().iter() }
    }

    pub(crate) fn child_at_range(
        &self,
        rel_range: TextRange,
    ) -> Option<(usize, TextSize, GreenElementRef<'_>)> {
        let idx = self
            .slice()
            .binary_search_by(|it| {
                let child_range = it.rel_range();
                TextRange::ordering(child_range, rel_range)
            })
            // XXX: this handles empty ranges
            .unwrap_or_else(|it| it.saturating_sub(1));
        let child = &self.slice().get(idx).filter(|it| it.rel_range().contains_range(rel_range))?;
        Some((idx, child.rel_offset(), child.as_ref()))
    }

    #[must_use]
    pub fn replace_child(&self, index: usize, new_child: GreenElement) -> GreenNode {
        let mut replacement = Some(new_child);
        let children = self.children().enumerate().map(|(i, child)| {
            if i == index {
                replacement.take().unwrap()
            } else {
                child.to_owned()
            }
        });
        GreenNode::new(self.kind(), children)
    }
    #[must_use]
    pub fn insert_child(&self, index: usize, new_child: GreenElement) -> GreenNode {
        // https://github.com/rust-lang/rust/issues/34433
        self.splice_children(index..index, iter::once(new_child))
    }
    #[must_use]
    pub fn remove_child(&self, index: usize) -> GreenNode {
        self.splice_children(index..=index, iter::empty())
    }
    #[must_use]
    pub fn splice_children<R, I>(&self, range: R, replace_with: I) -> GreenNode
    where
        R: ops::RangeBounds<usize>,
        I: IntoIterator<Item = GreenElement>,
    {
        let mut children: Vec<_> = self.children().map(|it| it.to_owned()).collect();
        children.splice(range, replace_with);
        GreenNode::new(self.kind(), children)
    }
}

impl ops::Deref for GreenNode {
    type Target = GreenNodeData;

    #[inline]
    fn deref(&self) -> &GreenNodeData {
        unsafe {
            let repr: &Repr = &self.ptr;
            let repr: &ReprThin = &*(repr as *const Repr as *const ReprThin);
            mem::transmute::<&ReprThin, &GreenNodeData>(repr)
        }
    }
}

impl GreenNode {
    /// Creates new Node.
    #[inline]
    pub fn new<I>(kind: SyntaxKind, children: I) -> GreenNode
    where
        I: IntoIterator<Item = GreenElement>,
        I::IntoIter: ExactSizeIterator,
    {
        let mut text_len: TextSize = 0.into();
        let children = children.into_iter().map(|el| {
            let rel_offset = text_len;
            text_len += el.text_len();
            match el {
                NodeOrToken::Node(node) => GreenChild::Node { rel_offset, node },
                NodeOrToken::Token(token) => GreenChild::Token { rel_offset, token },
            }
        });

        let data = ThinArc::from_header_and_iter(
            GreenNodeHead { kind, text_len: 0.into(), _c: Count::new() },
            children,
        );

        // XXX: fixup `text_len` after construction, because we can't iterate
        // `children` twice.
        let data = {
            let mut data = Arc::from_thin(data);
            Arc::get_mut(&mut data).unwrap().header.text_len = text_len;
            Arc::into_thin(data)
        };

        GreenNode { ptr: data }
    }

    #[inline]
    pub(crate) fn into_raw(this: GreenNode) -> ptr::NonNull<GreenNodeData> {
        let green = ManuallyDrop::new(this);
        let green: &GreenNodeData = &*green;
        ptr::NonNull::from(&*green)
    }

    #[inline]
    pub(crate) unsafe fn from_raw(ptr: ptr::NonNull<GreenNodeData>) -> GreenNode {
        let arc = Arc::from_raw(&ptr.as_ref().data as *const ReprThin);
        let arc = mem::transmute::<Arc<ReprThin>, ThinArc<GreenNodeHead, GreenChild>>(arc);
        GreenNode { ptr: arc }
    }
}

impl GreenChild {
    #[inline]
    pub(crate) fn as_ref(&self) -> GreenElementRef {
        match self {
            GreenChild::Node { node, .. } => NodeOrToken::Node(node),
            GreenChild::Token { token, .. } => NodeOrToken::Token(token),
        }
    }
    #[inline]
    pub(crate) fn rel_offset(&self) -> TextSize {
        match self {
            GreenChild::Node { rel_offset, .. } | GreenChild::Token { rel_offset, .. } => {
                *rel_offset
            }
        }
    }
    #[inline]
    fn rel_range(&self) -> TextRange {
        let len = self.as_ref().text_len();
        TextRange::at(self.rel_offset(), len)
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Children<'a> {
    pub(crate) raw: slice::Iter<'a, GreenChild>,
}

// NB: forward everything stable that iter::Slice specializes as of Rust 1.39.0
impl ExactSizeIterator for Children<'_> {
    #[inline(always)]
    fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.raw.len()
    }
}

impl<'a> Iterator for Children<'a> {
    type Item = GreenElementRef<'a>;

    #[inline]
    fn next(&mut self) -> Option<GreenElementRef<'a>> {
        self.raw.next().map(GreenChild::as_ref)
    }

    #[inline]
    fn size_hint(&self) -> (usize, Option<usize>) {
        self.raw.size_hint()
    }

    #[inline]
    fn count(self) -> usize
    where
        Self: Sized,
    {
        self.raw.count()
    }

    #[inline]
    fn nth(&mut self, n: usize) -> Option<Self::Item> {
        self.raw.nth(n).map(GreenChild::as_ref)
    }

    #[inline]
    fn last(mut self) -> Option<Self::Item>
    where
        Self: Sized,
    {
        self.next_back()
    }

    #[inline]
    fn fold<Acc, Fold>(mut self, init: Acc, mut f: Fold) -> Acc
    where
        Fold: FnMut(Acc, Self::Item) -> Acc,
    {
        let mut accum = init;
        while let Some(x) = self.next() {
            accum = f(accum, x);
        }
        accum
    }
}

impl<'a> DoubleEndedIterator for Children<'a> {
    #[inline]
    fn next_back(&mut self) -> Option<Self::Item> {
        self.raw.next_back().map(GreenChild::as_ref)
    }

    #[inline]
    fn nth_back(&mut self, n: usize) -> Option<Self::Item> {
        self.raw.nth_back(n).map(GreenChild::as_ref)
    }

    #[inline]
    fn rfold<Acc, Fold>(mut self, init: Acc, mut f: Fold) -> Acc
    where
        Fold: FnMut(Acc, Self::Item) -> Acc,
    {
        let mut accum = init;
        while let Some(x) = self.next_back() {
            accum = f(accum, x);
        }
        accum
    }
}

impl FusedIterator for Children<'_> {}

use hashbrown::hash_map::RawEntryMut;
use rustc_hash::FxHasher;
use std::hash::{BuildHasherDefault, Hash, Hasher};

use crate::{
    green::GreenElementRef, GreenNode, GreenNodeData, GreenToken, GreenTokenData, NodeOrToken,
    SyntaxKind,
};

use super::element::GreenElement;

type HashMap<K, V> = hashbrown::HashMap<K, V, BuildHasherDefault<FxHasher>>;

#[derive(Debug)]
struct NoHash<T>(T);

/// Interner for GreenTokens and GreenNodes
// XXX: the impl is a bit tricky. As usual when writing interners, we want to
// store all values in one HashSet.
//
// However, hashing trees is fun: hash of the tree is recursively defined. We
// maintain an invariant -- if the tree is interned, then all of its children
// are interned as well.
//
// That means that computing the hash naively is wasteful -- we just *know*
// hashes of children, and we can re-use those.
//
// So here we use *raw* API of hashbrown and provide the hashes manually,
// instead of going via a `Hash` impl. Our manual `Hash` and the
// `#[derive(Hash)]` are actually different! At some point we had a fun bug,
// where we accidentally mixed the two hashes, which made the cache much less
// efficient.
//
// To fix that, we additionally wrap the data in `NoHash` wrapper, to make sure
// we don't accidentally use the wrong hash!
#[derive(Default, Debug)]
pub struct NodeCache {
    nodes: HashMap<NoHash<GreenNode>, ()>,
    tokens: HashMap<NoHash<GreenToken>, ()>,
}

fn token_hash(token: &GreenTokenData) -> u64 {
    let mut h = FxHasher::default();
    token.kind().hash(&mut h);
    token.text().hash(&mut h);
    h.finish()
}

fn node_hash(node: &GreenNodeData) -> u64 {
    let mut h = FxHasher::default();
    node.kind().hash(&mut h);
    for child in node.children() {
        match child {
            NodeOrToken::Node(it) => node_hash(it),
            NodeOrToken::Token(it) => token_hash(it),
        }
        .hash(&mut h)
    }
    h.finish()
}

fn element_id(elem: GreenElementRef<'_>) -> *const () {
    match elem {
        NodeOrToken::Node(it) => it as *const GreenNodeData as *const (),
        NodeOrToken::Token(it) => it as *const GreenTokenData as *const (),
    }
}

impl NodeCache {
    pub(crate) fn node(
        &mut self,
        kind: SyntaxKind,
        children: &mut Vec<(u64, GreenElement)>,
        first_child: usize,
    ) -> (u64, GreenNode) {
        let build_node = move |children: &mut Vec<(u64, GreenElement)>| {
            GreenNode::new(kind, children.drain(first_child..).map(|(_, it)| it))
        };

        let children_ref = &children[first_child..];
        if children_ref.len() > 3 {
            let node = build_node(children);
            return (0, node);
        }

        let hash = {
            let mut h = FxHasher::default();
            kind.hash(&mut h);
            for &(hash, _) in children_ref {
                if hash == 0 {
                    let node = build_node(children);
                    return (0, node);
                }
                hash.hash(&mut h);
            }
            h.finish()
        };

        // Green nodes are fully immutable, so it's ok to deduplicate them.
        // This is the same optimization that Roslyn does
        // https://github.com/KirillOsenkov/Bliki/wiki/Roslyn-Immutable-Trees
        //
        // For example, all `#[inline]` in this file share the same green node!
        // For `libsyntax/parse/parser.rs`, measurements show that deduping saves
        // 17% of the memory for green nodes!
        let entry = self.nodes.raw_entry_mut().from_hash(hash, |node| {
            node.0.kind() == kind && node.0.children().len() == children_ref.len() && {
                let lhs = node.0.children();
                let rhs = children_ref.iter().map(|(_, it)| it.as_deref());

                let lhs = lhs.map(element_id);
                let rhs = rhs.map(element_id);

                lhs.eq(rhs)
            }
        });

        let node = match entry {
            RawEntryMut::Occupied(entry) => {
                drop(children.drain(first_child..));
                entry.key().0.clone()
            }
            RawEntryMut::Vacant(entry) => {
                let node = build_node(children);
                entry.insert_with_hasher(hash, NoHash(node.clone()), (), |n| node_hash(&n.0));
                node
            }
        };

        (hash, node)
    }

    pub(crate) fn token(&mut self, kind: SyntaxKind, text: &str) -> (u64, GreenToken) {
        let hash = {
            let mut h = FxHasher::default();
            kind.hash(&mut h);
            text.hash(&mut h);
            h.finish()
        };

        let entry = self
            .tokens
            .raw_entry_mut()
            .from_hash(hash, |token| token.0.kind() == kind && token.0.text() == text);

        let token = match entry {
            RawEntryMut::Occupied(entry) => entry.key().0.clone(),
            RawEntryMut::Vacant(entry) => {
                let token = GreenToken::new(kind, text);
                entry.insert_with_hasher(hash, NoHash(token.clone()), (), |t| token_hash(&t.0));
                token
            }
        };

        (hash, token)
    }
}

        &self.parse_root

impl std::fmt::Debug for CallerEvidenceBinding {
    fn fmt(&self, formatter: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {

            .finish()

            && self.declaration == other.declaration
            && self.identity == other.identity
            && Arc::ptr_eq(&self.payload, &other.payload)
    }

/// A stable definition identity within one source-root module.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, PartialEq)]
pub struct DefId {
    module: ModuleId,
    spelling: Box<str>,
    same_name_ordinal: u32,
}

impl DefId {
    pub fn module(&self) -> &ModuleId {
        &self.module
    }

    pub fn spelling(&self) -> &str {
        &self.spelling
    }

    pub fn same_name_ordinal(&self) -> u32 {
        self.same_name_ordinal
    }

    /// Returns the dynamic identity payload bytes that this `DefId`'s derived
    /// `Hash` and equality may scan.
    ///
    /// This includes the definition spelling plus the source-root module
    /// file-key realm and normalized workspace-relative path. It excludes
    /// fixed-size enum discriminants, string length metadata, pointer and
    /// container storage, and `same_name_ordinal`: those values participate in
    /// the derived operations but do not add a variable-length identity payload
    /// to scan.
    pub fn hash_eq_payload_bytes(&self) -> usize {
        let file_key = self.module.file().key();
        self.spelling.len()
            + file_key.realm().len()
            + file_key.normalized_workspace_relative_path().len()
    }
}

    pub fn index(self) -> u32 {
        self.0
    }
}

pub struct HirDiagnosticId(u32);

impl HirDiagnosticId {
    pub fn index(self) -> u32 {
        self.0
    }
}

/// A lowering-order expression identity branded by its immutable HIR artifact.
///
/// The token is intentionally private and non-serializable. An ordinal is only
/// meaningful alongside this token, so independently lowered modules cannot
/// accidentally use each other's occurrence zero.
#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct HirOccurrenceId {
    artifact: Arc<HirArtifactToken>,
    ordinal: u32,
}

/// An admitted definition identity branded by the immutable HIR artifact that
/// owns it. The definition payload is shared with its binding so copying a
/// root never copies a `DefId` payload.
#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct DefinitionRootId {
    artifact: Arc<HirArtifactToken>,
    definition: Arc<DefId>,
}

impl DefinitionRootId {
    fn new(artifact: Arc<HirArtifactToken>, definition: Arc<DefId>) -> Self {
        Self {
            artifact,
            definition,
        }
    }
}

impl std::fmt::Debug for DefinitionRootId {
    fn fmt(&self, formatter: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        formatter
            .debug_struct("DefinitionRootId")
            .finish_non_exhaustive()
    }
}

impl PartialEq for DefinitionRootId {
    fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {
        Arc::ptr_eq(&self.artifact, &other.artifact) && self.definition == other.definition
    }
}

impl Eq for DefinitionRootId {}

impl Hash for DefinitionRootId {
    fn hash<H: Hasher>(&self, state: &mut H) {
        Arc::as_ptr(&self.artifact).hash(state);
        self.definition.hash(state);
    }
}

pub struct ExpressionRootId {
    occurrence: HirOccurrenceId,
}
impl ExpressionRootId {
    pub fn occurrence(&self) -> &HirOccurrenceId { &self.occurrence }
}

pub fn lower_module(
    identity: ModuleIdentity,
    parsed: &ParsedFile,
    imports: SemanticImports,
) -> Result<HirModule, HirAvailabilityError> {
    let mut counters = LoweringCounters::default();
    counters.source_identity = Some(crate::source_identity::HirSourceIdentity::new(parsed));
    counters.source_nodes = crate::source_identity::try_source_keys(parsed)?;
    counters.ordinary_source_carriers = true;
    lower_module_with_counters(identity, parsed, imports, &mut counters)
}

#[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
pub(crate) fn lower_module_with_selected_member_sources(
    identity: ModuleIdentity,
    parsed: &ParsedFile,
    imports: SemanticImports,
    selected_members: std::collections::HashSet<yu_syntax::SourceNodeKey>,
) -> Result<HirModule, HirAvailabilityError> {
    if selected_members.iter().any(|member| !parsed.owns_source_key(member)) {
        return Err(HirAvailabilityError::ForeignSourceImport);
    }
    let mut counters = LoweringCounters::default();
    counters.source_identity = Some(crate::source_identity::HirSourceIdentity::new(parsed));
    counters.source_nodes = crate::source_identity::source_keys(parsed);
    counters.member_sources = true;
    counters.selected_member_sources = Some(selected_members);
    // A member-only provider contributes its exact member carrier, not the
    // unrelated direct module roots from the file that happened to contain it.
    counters.selected_local_source_roots = Some(std::collections::HashSet::new());
    lower_module_with_counters(identity, parsed, imports, &mut counters)
}

#[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
pub(crate) fn lower_module_with_selected_local_source(
    identity: ModuleIdentity,
    parsed: &ParsedFile,
    imports: SemanticImports,
    selected_roots: std::collections::HashSet<yu_syntax::SourceNodeKey>,
) -> Result<HirModule, HirAvailabilityError> {
    if selected_roots.iter().any(|root| !parsed.owns_source_key(root)) {
        return Err(HirAvailabilityError::ForeignSourceImport);
    }
    let mut counters = LoweringCounters::default();
    counters.source_identity = Some(crate::source_identity::HirSourceIdentity::new(parsed));
    counters.source_nodes = crate::source_identity::source_keys(parsed);
    counters.local_source = true;
    counters.selected_local_source_roots = Some(selected_roots);
    lower_module_with_counters(identity, parsed, imports, &mut counters)
}

#[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
pub(crate) fn index_role_impl_sources(
    identity: ModuleIdentity,
    parsed: &ParsedFile,
) -> Result<(
    Vec<source_role::SourceRoleDeclaration>,
    Vec<source_impl::SourceImplementationDeclaration>,
), HirAvailabilityError> {
    if identity.file != *identity.module.file() {
        return Err(HirAvailabilityError::InconsistentIdentity);
    }
    let mut counters = LoweringCounters::default();
    counters.source_nodes = crate::source_identity::try_source_keys(parsed)?;
    let root = SyntaxNode::new_root(parsed.green().clone());
    let roles = source_role::declarations(&root, identity.module(), &counters)?;
    let implementations = source_impl::declarations(&root, identity.module(), &counters)?;
    Ok((roles, implementations))
}

#[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
pub(crate) fn index_source_effects(
    identity: ModuleIdentity,
    parsed: &ParsedFile,
    selected: &std::collections::HashSet<yu_syntax::SourceNodeKey>,
) -> Result<Vec<source_annotation::SourceEffectDeclaration>, HirAvailabilityError> {
    if identity.file != *identity.module.file() {
        return Err(HirAvailabilityError::InconsistentIdentity);
    }
    let mut counters = LoweringCounters::default();
    counters.source_nodes = crate::source_identity::try_source_keys(parsed)?;
    let root = SyntaxNode::new_root(parsed.green().clone());
    source_annotation::declarations_selected(&root, identity.module(), &mut counters, Some(selected))
        .map_err(Into::into)
}

pub fn index_source_exports(
    identity: ModuleIdentity,
    parsed: &ParsedFile,
) -> Result<source_exports::SourceExportIndex, HirAvailabilityError> {
    if identity.file != *identity.module.file() {
        return Err(HirAvailabilityError::InconsistentIdentity);
    }
    let mut counters = LoweringCounters::default();
    counters.source_nodes = crate::source_identity::try_source_keys(parsed)?;
    let root = SyntaxNode::new_root(parsed.green().clone());
    source_exports::index(
        &root,
        identity.module(),
        parsed.revision(),
        parsed.header().operators(),
        &counters,
    )
}

fn lower_module_with_counters(
    identity: ModuleIdentity,
    parsed: &ParsedFile,
    imports: SemanticImports,
    counters: &mut LoweringCounters,
) -> Result<HirModule, HirAvailabilityError> {
    if identity.file != *identity.module.file() {
        return Err(HirAvailabilityError::InconsistentIdentity);
    }
    if let Some(binding) = imports.caller_evidence.as_ref() {
        if binding.identity != identity {
            return Err(HirAvailabilityError::ForeignCallerEvidenceModule);
        }
        if !parsed.owns_source_key(&binding.parse_root)
            || binding.parse_root != parsed.source_root().key()
            || !parsed.owns_source_key(&binding.declaration)
        {
            return Err(HirAvailabilityError::ForeignCallerEvidenceSource);
        }
    }
    if let Some(source_imports) = imports.source_imports.as_ref() {
        if source_imports.identity() != &identity {
            return Err(HirAvailabilityError::ForeignSourceImport);
        }
        if source_imports.parse_root() != &parsed.source_root().key()
            || !parsed.owns_source_key(source_imports.parse_root())
        {
            return Err(HirAvailabilityError::ForeignSourceImport);
        }
    }
    counters.string_concat = imports.source_imports.as_ref().and_then(|imports| imports.string_concat())
        .map(source_exports::SourceExportResolution::identity);
    let recoveries = validated_recoveries(parsed)?;
    let root = SyntaxNode::new_root(parsed.green().clone());
    if counters.ordinary_source_carriers || counters.local_source || counters.member_sources {
        let associations = crate::associate_operator_chains(parsed);
        let mut uses = HashMap::new();
        let mut chains = HashMap::new();
        for chain in associations.chains() {
            retain_associated_operator_uses(chain.expression(), &mut uses)?;
            let tree = Arc::new(chain.expression().clone());
            retain_associated_operator_chains(&tree, &tree, &mut chains)?;
        }
        counters.source_operator_uses = uses;
        counters.source_associated_chains = chains;
    }
    #[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
    if counters.local_source {
        counters.effect_declarations = if counters.selected_local_source_roots.is_some() {
            // A selected provider root carries exact imported operations in
            // SourceImportEnvironment::operation_paths. Scanning every Act in
            // the provider file would make an unrelated host declaration a
            // prerequisite for retaining that binding's LocalSource.
            let no_local_effects = std::collections::HashSet::new();
            source_annotation::declarations_selected(
                &root, identity.module(), counters, Some(&no_local_effects),
            )?
        } else {
            source_annotation::declarations(&root, identity.module(), counters)?
        };
    }
    #[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
    if counters.local_source || counters.member_sources {
        counters.role_declarations =
            source_role::declarations(&root, identity.module(), counters)?;
        counters.implementation_declarations =
            source_impl::declarations(&root, identity.module(), counters)?;
    }
    let mut plans = Vec::new();
    for (index, node) in root.children().enumerate() {
        let ordinal = u32::try_from(index).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
        let mut plan = plan_root(node, ordinal, &identity, counters)?;
        if plan.node.kind() == SyntaxKind::UseDeclaration
            && !has_recovery(&plan.node)
            && imports.source_imports.as_ref().is_some_and(|environment| environment.complete_source_uses)
        {
            plan.kind = RootPlanKind::Import;
        }
        plans.push(plan);
    }
    let selected_ordinals = if let Some(selected) = counters.selected_local_source_roots.as_ref() {
        let mut ordinals = std::collections::HashSet::new();
        ordinals.try_reserve(selected.len()).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
        for plan in &plans {
            if counters.source_nodes.get(&plan.node).is_some_and(|source| selected.contains(source)) {
                ordinals.insert(plan.ordinal);
            }
        }
        if ordinals.len() != selected.len() {
            return Err(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection);
        }
        Some(ordinals)
    } else { None };
    let partition = partition_recoveries(&recoveries, plans.len(), counters)?;
    let namespace = namespace(&mut plans, imports.source_imports.as_ref())?;
    let mut sink = ErrorSink::default();
    let root_errors = emit_structural_errors(&recoveries, &partition, &plans, &mut sink, counters)?;

    #[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
    if counters.local_source {
        for (declaration, plan) in counters.effect_declarations.iter().zip(
            plans
                .iter()
                .filter(|plan| plan.node.kind() == SyntaxKind::ActDeclaration),
        ) {
            let ordinal = plan.ordinal;
            if counters
                .effect_namespace
                .get(declaration.spelling.as_ref())
                .is_some_and(|ids| ids.len() > 1)
            {
                sink.lowering(
                    HirErrorKind::DuplicateDefinition,
                    HirErrorAttachment::DirectRootItem(ordinal),
                    declaration.range.clone(),
                )?;
            }
            for member in &declaration.operations {
                if counters
                    .operation_namespace
                    .get(&(declaration.id.clone(), member.spelling.clone()))
                    .is_some_and(|members| members.len() > 1)
                {
                    sink.lowering(
                        HirErrorKind::DuplicateDefinition,
                        HirErrorAttachment::DirectRootItem(ordinal),
                        member.range.clone(),
                    )?;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    let artifact = mint_artifact_token();
    let mut items = Vec::with_capacity(plans.len());
    let mut expression_roots = HashMap::new();
    let mut next_occurrence_ordinal = 0u32;
    let mut scope = ScopeStack::default();
    #[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
    let mut effect_ordinal = 0usize;
    for (plan, errors) in plans.iter().zip(root_errors) {
        let occurrence = next_occurrence(&artifact, &mut next_occurrence_ordinal)?;
        if matches!(plan.kind, RootPlanKind::DirectExpression) {
            expression_roots.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
            let root = ExpressionRootId { occurrence: occurrence.clone() };
            if let Some(source) = &mut counters.source_identity {
                let key = counters.source_nodes.get(&plan.node)
                    .ok_or(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection)?;
                source.expressions.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
                source.expressions.insert(root.clone(), key.clone());
            }
            expression_roots.insert(occurrence.clone(), root);
        }
        let definition_root = match &plan.kind {
            RootPlanKind::Binding(admitted) => {
                Some(DefinitionRootId::new(artifact.clone(), admitted.id.clone()))
            }
            RootPlanKind::Import | RootPlanKind::DirectExpression | RootPlanKind::Unsupported(_) => None,
        };
        if let (Some(source), Some(definition)) = (&mut counters.source_identity, &definition_root)
        {
            if let Some(key) = counters.source_nodes.get(&plan.node) {
                source.record_definition(definition.clone(), key.clone());
            }
        }
        if definition_root.is_some() {
            counters.definition_root_allocation_bytes += std::mem::size_of::<DefinitionRootId>();
        }
        let item = lower_plan(
            plan,
            parsed,
            &namespace,
            errors.item,
            errors.value,
            &mut sink,
            counters,
            occurrence,
            definition_root,
            &artifact,
            &mut next_occurrence_ordinal,
            &mut scope,
        )?;
        #[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
        if counters.local_source
            && counters.selected_local_source_roots.is_none()
            && plan.node.kind() == SyntaxKind::ActDeclaration
        {
            let declaration = counters
                .effect_declarations
                .get_mut(effect_ordinal)
                .ok_or(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection)?;
            effect_ordinal += 1;
            if let HirItem::Error { errors, .. } = &item {
                declaration
                    .placeholder_errors
                    .extend(errors.iter().copied().filter(|id| {
                        sink.errors.iter().any(|error| {
                            error.id == *id && error.kind == HirErrorKind::UnsupportedItem
                        })
                    }));
            }
        }
        items.push(item);
    }
    // Binding carrier allocation stays a complete first pass, preserving all
    // previously published binding-carrier ordinals in mixed modules.
    let mut outcomes = Vec::new();
    outcomes.try_reserve_exact(plans.len()).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
    outcomes.resize_with(plans.len(), || None);
    if counters.ordinary_source_carriers {
        for (index, (plan, item)) in plans.iter().zip(&items).enumerate() {
            let HirItem::Binding(binding) = item else { continue; };
            if counters.selected_local_source_roots.as_ref().is_some_and(|selected| {
                counters.source_nodes.get(&plan.node).is_none_or(|source| !selected.contains(source))
            }) { continue; }
            let owner = SourceRootId::Definition(binding.definition_root.clone());
            let outcome = local_source::form_ordinary(
                plan, &owner, &binding.parameters, &namespace,
                counters, &artifact, &mut next_occurrence_ordinal,
            )?;
            outcomes[index] = Some((owner, outcome));
        }
    }
    if counters.ordinary_source_carriers || counters.local_source {
        for (index, (plan, item)) in plans.iter().zip(&items).enumerate() {
            let HirItem::Expression(expression) = item else { continue; };
            if counters.selected_local_source_roots.as_ref().is_some_and(|selected| {
                counters.source_nodes.get(&plan.node).is_none_or(|source| !selected.contains(source))
            }) { continue; }
            let root = expression_roots.get(expression.occurrence())
                .ok_or(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection)?;
            let owner = SourceRootId::Expression(root.clone());
            let outcome = local_source::form_ordinary(
                plan, &owner, &[], &namespace, counters, &artifact, &mut next_occurrence_ordinal,
            )?;
            outcomes[index] = Some((owner, outcome));
        }
    }
    #[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
    let source_member_carriers = if counters.member_sources
        || (counters.local_source && counters.selected_local_source_roots.is_none())
    {
        let mut carriers = Vec::new();
        let mut selected_member_found = std::collections::HashSet::new();
        if let Some(selected) = &counters.selected_member_sources {
            selected_member_found.try_reserve(selected.len())
                .map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
        }
        // Carrier construction mutates lowering counters, so walk owned
        // declaration snapshots rather than aliasing the declaration tables.
        let role_declarations = counters.role_declarations.clone();
        let implementation_declarations = counters.implementation_declarations.clone();
        for declaration in &role_declarations {
            let Some(body) = declaration.body.as_ref() else { continue; };
            for member in &body.members {
                if member.recovered { continue; }
                let selected_member = counters.selected_member_sources.as_ref().is_some_and(|selected|
                    selected.contains(&member.id.declaration));
                if counters.selected_member_sources.is_some() && !selected_member { continue; }
                let Some(header) = member.header.as_ref() else { continue; };
                let syntax = crate::source_identity::syntax_node_at_path(parsed, &member.source_path)?;
                if syntax.kind() != member.kind
                    || crate::range_of(&syntax) != member.range
                    || syntax.text().to_string() != member.text.as_ref()
                    || counters.source_nodes.get(&syntax) != Some(&member.id.declaration)
                { return Err(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection); }
                let Some(expression) = local_source::member_body_expression(&syntax)? else {
                    if selected_member { return Err(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection); }
                    continue;
                };
                let owner = SourceRootId::Member(SourceMemberRootId::new(
                    artifact.clone(), identity.module().clone(),
                    declaration.id.declaration.clone(), member.id.declaration.clone(),
                    SourceMemberKind::Role,
                ));
                let plan = RootPlan { ordinal: 0, range: crate::range_of(&expression), node: expression, kind: RootPlanKind::DirectExpression };
                let outcome = local_source::form_member(
                    &plan, &owner, header, &namespace, counters, &artifact, &mut next_occurrence_ordinal,
                )?;
                carriers.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
                carriers.push((owner, outcome));
                if selected_member && !selected_member_found.insert(member.id.declaration.clone()) {
                    return Err(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection);
                }
            }
        }
        for declaration in &implementation_declarations {
            let Some(body) = declaration.body.as_ref() else { continue; };
            for member in body.entries.iter().filter(|entry| entry.member.is_some()) {
                if member.recovered { continue; }
                let selected_member = counters.selected_member_sources.as_ref().is_some_and(|selected|
                    selected.contains(&member.source));
                if counters.selected_member_sources.is_some() && !selected_member { continue; }
                let Some(header) = member.header.as_ref() else { continue; };
                let path = member.source_path.as_deref().ok_or(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection)?;
                let syntax = crate::source_identity::syntax_node_at_path(parsed, path)?;
                if syntax.kind() != member.kind
                    || crate::range_of(&syntax) != member.range
                    || syntax.text().to_string() != member.text.as_ref()
                    || counters.source_nodes.get(&syntax) != Some(&member.source)
                { return Err(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection); }
                let Some(expression) = local_source::member_body_expression(&syntax)? else {
                    if selected_member { return Err(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection); }
                    continue;
                };
                let owner = SourceRootId::Member(SourceMemberRootId::new(
                    artifact.clone(), identity.module().clone(),
                    declaration.id.declaration.clone(), member.source.clone(),
                    SourceMemberKind::Implementation,
                ));
                let plan = RootPlan { ordinal: 0, range: crate::range_of(&expression), node: expression, kind: RootPlanKind::DirectExpression };
                let outcome = local_source::form_member(
                    &plan, &owner, header, &namespace, counters, &artifact, &mut next_occurrence_ordinal,
                )?;
                carriers.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
                carriers.push((owner, outcome));
                if selected_member && !selected_member_found.insert(member.source.clone()) {
                    return Err(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection);
                }
            }
        }
        if counters.selected_member_sources.as_ref().is_some_and(|selected|
            selected_member_found.len() != selected.len()) {
            return Err(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection);
        }
        carriers
    } else { Vec::new() };
    #[cfg(not(any(feature = "shadow", test)))]
    let source_member_carriers: Vec<(SourceRootId, local_source::SourceCarrierOutcome)> = Vec::new();
    finalize_ordinary_admissions(
        &mut items,
        counters,
        &artifact,
        &mut next_occurrence_ordinal,
    )?;
    if let Some(selected) = &selected_ordinals {
        let retained_definitions = plans.iter().filter_map(|plan| {
            if !selected.contains(&plan.ordinal) { return None; }
            match &plan.kind { RootPlanKind::Binding(admitted) => Some(admitted.id.clone()), _ => None }
        }).collect::<std::collections::HashSet<_>>();
        let retained_errors = sink.errors.iter().filter(|error| match error.attachment() {
            HirErrorAttachment::Module => plans.iter().any(|plan| {
                selected.contains(&plan.ordinal)
                    && (error.range.start < plan.range.end && plan.range.start < error.range.end
                        || error.range.is_empty() && plan.range.start <= error.range.start && error.range.start <= plan.range.end)
            }),
            HirErrorAttachment::DirectRootItem(ordinal) => selected.contains(ordinal),
            HirErrorAttachment::Definition(id) | HirErrorAttachment::Value(id) => retained_definitions.contains(id),
        }).map(HirError::id).collect::<std::collections::HashSet<_>>();
        sink.errors.retain(|error| retained_errors.contains(&error.id()));
        sink.diagnostics.retain(|diagnostic| retained_errors.contains(&diagnostic.error()));
        let mut ordinal = 0u32;
        items.retain(|_| { let keep = selected.contains(&ordinal); ordinal += 1; keep });
        expression_roots.retain(|occurrence, _| items.iter().any(|item| matches!(item, HirItem::Expression(expression) if expression.occurrence() == occurrence)));
        let source_nodes = &counters.source_nodes;
        if let Some(source) = counters.source_identity.as_mut() {
            source.definitions.retain(|_, key| plans.iter().any(|plan| {
                selected.contains(&plan.ordinal) && source_nodes.get(&plan.node).is_some_and(|root| root == key)
            }));
            source.expressions.retain(|_, key| plans.iter().any(|plan| {
                selected.contains(&plan.ordinal) && source_nodes.get(&plan.node).is_some_and(|root| root == key)
            }));
        }
    }
    let source_carriers: Vec<_> = outcomes.into_iter().flatten().collect();
    let mut source_carrier_indices = HashMap::new();
    source_carrier_indices.try_reserve(source_carriers.len()).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
    for (index, (owner, _)) in source_carriers.iter().enumerate() {
        source_carrier_indices.insert(owner.clone(), index);
    }
    let mut source_member_carrier_indices = HashMap::new();
    source_member_carrier_indices.try_reserve(source_member_carriers.len()).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
    for (index, (owner, _)) in source_member_carriers.iter().enumerate() {
        source_member_carrier_indices.insert(owner.clone(), index);
    }
    if let Some(binding) = imports.caller_evidence.as_ref() {
        let admitted = counters.source_identity.as_ref().is_some_and(|source| {
            source.definitions.values().any(|key| key == &binding.declaration)
        });
        if !admitted {
            return Err(HirAvailabilityError::CallerEvidenceNotDeclaration);
        }
    }
    Ok(HirModule {
        expression_roots,
        source_carriers,
        source_carrier_indices,
        source_member_carriers,
        source_member_carrier_indices,
        artifact,
        caller_evidence: imports.caller_evidence,
        source_imports: imports.source_imports,
        source_identity: counters.source_identity.take(),
        #[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
        captured_source: None,
        source_parsed: (counters.local_source || counters.member_sources).then(|| parsed.clone()),
        local_sources: std::mem::take(&mut counters.local_sources),
        #[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
        effect_declarations: std::mem::take(&mut counters.effect_declarations),
        #[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
        role_declarations: std::mem::take(&mut counters.role_declarations),
        #[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
        implementation_declarations: std::mem::take(&mut counters.implementation_declarations),
        identity,
        source_revision: parsed.revision(),
        items,
        errors: sink.errors,
        diagnostics: sink.diagnostics,
        definition_root_allocation_bytes: counters.definition_root_allocation_bytes,
        definition_root_def_id_clone_bytes: counters.definition_root_def_id_clone_bytes,
    })
}
===== END R274-E022; omitted surrounding regions are NOT evidence or assumed no-ops =====
===== R274-E023 | crates/yu-hir/src/module/local_source.rs:968-2835 | source sha256 7055975d582dfb198836c2aa659f8b8033c29dff419d522feea4fea4c42523d1 | excerpt sha256 4d505e4f296ffa22a81d265703d6ba6c41494dd30e1d793db0d9e1fa2441081e | local-source-carrier-and-builders =====
pub(super) fn form_ordinary(
    plan: &RootPlan,
    owner: &SourceRootId,
    parameters: &[HirParameter],
    namespace: &super::ModuleNamespace,
    counters: &mut LoweringCounters,
    artifact: &Arc<HirArtifactToken>,
    next_occurrence: &mut u32,
) -> Result<SourceCarrierOutcome, HirAvailabilityError> {
    form_ordinary_with_root_parameters(
        plan, owner, parameters, &[], None, namespace, counters, artifact, next_occurrence,
    )
}

pub(super) fn form_member(
    plan: &RootPlan,
    owner: &SourceRootId,
    header: &super::SourceMemberHeader,
    namespace: &super::ModuleNamespace,
    counters: &mut LoweringCounters,
    artifact: &Arc<HirArtifactToken>,
    next_occurrence: &mut u32,
) -> Result<SourceCarrierOutcome, HirAvailabilityError> {
    let root = owner.member_root().ok_or(HirAvailabilityError::InconsistentIdentity)?;
    let receiver = LocalSourceParameter {
        id: super::HirParameterId::new(owner.clone(), 0),
        source: header.receiver.source.clone(),
        range: header.receiver.range.clone(),
        spelling: header.receiver.spelling.clone(),
        scope: LocalSourceScope::SourceMember(root.clone()),
        annotation: None,
        unit_pattern: false,
        record_pattern: None,
    };
    let mut parameters = Vec::new();
    parameters.try_reserve_exact(header.parameters.len())
        .map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
    for (index, parameter) in header.parameters.iter().enumerate() {
        let ordinal = u32::try_from(index.checked_add(1)
            .ok_or(HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?)
            .map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
        parameters.push(HirParameter {
            id: super::HirParameterId::new(owner.clone(), ordinal),
            name: super::HirName {
                spelling: parameter.spelling.to_string(),
                range: parameter.range.clone(),
            },
            range: parameter.range.clone(),
            unit_pattern: false,
            record_pattern: false,
        });
    }
    form_ordinary_with_root_parameters(
        plan, owner, &parameters, &[receiver], Some(header), namespace, counters, artifact, next_occurrence,
    )
}

fn form_ordinary_with_root_parameters(
    plan: &RootPlan,
    owner: &SourceRootId,
    parameters: &[HirParameter],
    root_parameters: &[LocalSourceParameter],
    member_header: Option<&super::SourceMemberHeader>,
    namespace: &super::ModuleNamespace,
    counters: &mut LoweringCounters,
    artifact: &Arc<HirArtifactToken>,
    next_occurrence: &mut u32,
) -> Result<SourceCarrierOutcome, HirAvailabilityError> {
    let mut identity = counters.source_identity.take()
        .ok_or(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection)?;
    let source = counters.source_nodes.get(&plan.node).cloned()
        .ok_or(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection)?;
    counters.source_identity = Some(identity.empty_stage());
    let mut staged_next = *next_occurrence;
    // Only local candidate headers use this grammar switch. All ordinary
    // admission, lowering, errors and occurrence allocation have completed.
    let previous_local_source = counters.local_source;
    counters.local_source = true;
    let result = form(
        plan, owner, parameters, root_parameters, member_header, namespace, counters, artifact, &mut staged_next,
    );
    counters.local_source = previous_local_source;
    let mut staged_identity = counters.source_identity.take()
        .ok_or(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection)?;
    let outcome = match result {
        Ok((mut carrier, _)) => {
            staged_next = *next_occurrence;
            carrier.order_occurrences(plan, counters, artifact, &mut staged_next, &mut staged_identity)?;
            if let SourceRootId::Expression(root) = owner {
                if let Some(existing) = identity.occurrences.get(root.occurrence()) {
                    if staged_identity.occurrences.get(root.occurrence()) != Some(existing) {
                        counters.source_identity = Some(identity);
                        return Err(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection);
                    }
                    // This authentic ordinary occurrence already has this exact
                    // source origin; reuse it rather than replacing its mapping.
                    staged_identity.occurrences.remove(root.occurrence());
                }
            }
            let merged = identity.append_stage(staged_identity);
            counters.source_identity = Some(identity);
            merged?;
            *next_occurrence = staged_next;
            return Ok(SourceCarrierOutcome::Supported(carrier));
        }
        Err(FormationError::Unsupported(reason)) => {
            Ok(SourceCarrierOutcome::Unsupported(SourceCarrierFailure {
                root: owner.clone(), source, reason,
            }))
        }
        Err(FormationError::Availability(error)) => Err(error),
    };
    counters.source_identity = Some(identity);
    outcome
}

pub(super) fn form(
    plan: &RootPlan,
    owner: &SourceRootId,
    parameters: &[HirParameter],
    root_parameters: &[LocalSourceParameter],
    member_header: Option<&super::SourceMemberHeader>,
    namespace: &super::ModuleNamespace,
    counters: &mut LoweringCounters,
    artifact: &Arc<HirArtifactToken>,
    next_occurrence: &mut u32,
) -> Result<(LocalSource, SimpleBodyProjection), FormationError> {
    if has_recovery(&plan.node) {
        return Err(FormationError::Unsupported(SourceCarrierFailureReason::Recovery));
    }
    if parameters.len() >= 128 {
        return Err(FormationError::Unsupported(SourceCarrierFailureReason::SupportedDepth));
    }
    let admitted = match &plan.kind {
        RootPlanKind::Binding(admitted) => Some(admitted),
        RootPlanKind::DirectExpression => None,
        RootPlanKind::Import | RootPlanKind::Unsupported(_) => return Err(invalid()),
    };
    let admitted_parameters = admitted.map_or(&[][..], |admitted| admitted.parameters.as_slice());
    let mut builder = Builder {
        owner,
        namespace,
        counters,
        artifact,
        next_occurrence,
        next_parameter: u32::try_from(parameters.len().checked_add(root_parameters.len())
            .ok_or(HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?)
            .map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?,
        default_nodes: HashMap::new(),
        expressions: Vec::new(),
        named_groups: Vec::new(),
        bindings: Vec::new(),
        where_clauses: Vec::new(),
        lexical: Vec::new(),
        latest: HashMap::new(),
        work: Vec::new(),
    };
    for parameter in root_parameters {
        builder.counters.source_identity.as_mut()
            .ok_or(HirAvailabilityError::StructuralProjection)?
            .record_parameter(parameter.id.clone(), parameter.source.clone());
        builder.push_binding(
            parameter.spelling.clone(),
            LocalSourceResolution::Parameter(parameter.id.clone()),
        )?;
    }
    let root = builder.slot()?;
    let mut current = root.clone();
    let mut scope = root_parameters.first()
        .map(|parameter| LocalSourceScope::Parameter(parameter.id.clone()))
        .unwrap_or_else(|| match owner {
        SourceRootId::Definition(root) => LocalSourceScope::Definition(root.clone()),
        SourceRootId::Expression(root) => LocalSourceScope::ModuleExpression(root.clone()),
        SourceRootId::Member(root) => LocalSourceScope::SourceMember(root.clone()),
    });
    let named_group = if parameters.is_empty() { None } else { Some(builder.next_named_group()?) };
    let mut named_formals = Vec::new();
    let mut parameter_work = Vec::new();
    for (index, parameter) in parameters.iter().enumerate() {
        let (parameter_source, parameter_annotation) = if let Some(admitted) = admitted_parameters.get(index) {
            (admitted.source.clone().ok_or_else(invalid)?, admitted.annotation.as_ref())
        } else if let Some(source_parameter) = member_header.and_then(|header|
            header.parameters.get(index)
        ) {
            (source_parameter.source.clone(), None)
        } else {
            return Err(invalid());
        };
        let record_pattern = admitted_parameters.get(index).and_then(|parameter| parameter.record_pattern.as_ref())
            .map(|node| builder.record_parameter_pattern(node, None)).transpose()?;
        let parameter = LocalSourceParameter {
            id: parameter.id.clone(),
            source: parameter_source,
            range: parameter.range.clone(),
            spelling: parameter.name.spelling.clone().into_boxed_str(),
            unit_pattern: parameter.unit_pattern,
            record_pattern,
            scope: scope.clone(),
            annotation: parameter_annotation
                .map(|node| source_annotation::parse_annotation(node, owner, builder.counters))
                .transpose()?,
        };
        builder.parameter_work(&parameter, named_group.clone(), &mut parameter_work)?;
        let next = builder.slot()?;
        push(&mut named_formals, LocalSourceNamedFormal { parameter: parameter.id.clone(), lambda: current.clone() })?;
        builder.set(
            current,
            &plan.node,
            scope,
            named_group.clone(),
            LocalSourceForm::Lambda {
                parameter: parameter.clone(),
                body: next.clone(),
            },
        )?;
        current = next;
        scope = LocalSourceScope::Parameter(parameter.id);
    }
    if let Some(id) = &named_group {
        let group_owner = match owner {
            SourceRootId::Definition(root) => LocalSourceNamedGroupOwner::Definition(root.clone()),
            SourceRootId::Member(root) => LocalSourceNamedGroupOwner::Member(root.clone()),
            SourceRootId::Expression(_) => return Err(invalid()),
        };
        builder.record_named_group(id.clone(), group_owner, &plan.node,
            None, named_formals, current.clone())?;
    }
    let body_chain = if let Some(admitted) = admitted {
        body(&plan.node, admitted.visibility)?
    } else {
        plan.node.clone()
    };
    if body_chain.kind() == SyntaxKind::IndentedStatementBlock {
        for clause in body_chain.children() {
            if clause.kind() != SyntaxKind::WhereClause {
                break;
            }
            if has_recovery(&clause) {
                return Err(FormationError::Unsupported(SourceCarrierFailureReason::Recovery));
            }
            let source = builder
                .counters
                .source_nodes
                .get(&clause)
                .cloned()
                .ok_or_else(invalid)?;
            let mut parts = clause.children_with_tokens().filter(|element| !matches!(
                element.kind(), SyntaxKind::Whitespace | SyntaxKind::Newline
                    | SyntaxKind::LineComment | SyntaxKind::BlockComment,
            ));
            if parts.next().is_none_or(|part| part.kind() != SyntaxKind::WhereKw) {
                return Err(unsupported());
            }
            let subject = parts.next().and_then(|part| part.into_node())
                .filter(|node| node.kind() == SyntaxKind::TypeExpression)
                .ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
            if parts.next().is_none_or(|part| part.kind() != SyntaxKind::Colon) {
                return Err(unsupported());
            }
            let role = parts.next().and_then(|part| part.into_node())
                .filter(|node| node.kind() == SyntaxKind::TypeExpression)
                .ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
            if parts.next().is_some() {
                return Err(unsupported());
            }
            let retain_type = |node: &SyntaxNode| -> Result<LocalSourceWhereType, FormationError> {
                Ok(LocalSourceWhereType {
                    source: builder.counters.source_nodes.get(node).cloned().ok_or_else(invalid)?,
                    range: crate::range_of(node),
                    text: node.text().to_string().into_boxed_str(),
                })
            };
            let subject = retain_type(&subject)?;
            let role = retain_type(&role)?;
            push(
                &mut builder.where_clauses,
                LocalSourceWhereClause {
                    source,
                    range: crate::range_of(&clause),
                    text: clause.text().to_string().into_boxed_str(),
                    subject,
                    role,
                },
            )?;
        }
    }
    push(
        &mut builder.work,
        Work::Expression(body_chain.clone(), current, scope, named_group.clone()),
    )?;
    for work in parameter_work.into_iter().rev() { push(&mut builder.work, work)?; }
    while let Some(work) = builder.work.pop() {
        match work {
            Work::Expression(node, index, scope, named_group) => {
                builder.expression(node, index, scope, named_group)?
            }
            Work::Restore(depth) => builder.restore(depth),
            Work::BindParameter(id, spelling) => builder.push_binding(spelling, LocalSourceResolution::Parameter(id))?,
            Work::CatchArm(node, index, scope, binders, named_group) => {
                push(&mut builder.work, Work::Restore(builder.lexical.len()))?;
                for binder in binders {
                    builder.push_binding(binder.spelling, LocalSourceResolution::CatchBinder(binder.id))?;
                }
                push(&mut builder.work, Work::Expression(node, index, scope, named_group.clone()))?;
            }
            Work::CaseArm(node, index, scope, binders, named_group) => {
                push(&mut builder.work, Work::Restore(builder.lexical.len()))?;
                for binder in binders {
                    builder.push_binding(binder.spelling, LocalSourceResolution::CaseBinder(binder.id))?;
                }
                push(&mut builder.work, Work::Expression(node, index, scope, named_group.clone()))?;
            }
            Work::Initializer(binding, node, enclosing_named_group) => {
                let guard = builder.lexical.len();
                let data = &builder.bindings[binding as usize];
                let mut scope = LocalSourceScope::LocalInitializer(data.id.clone());
                let mut index = data.initializer.clone();
                let count = data.parameters.len();
                let group_owner = data.id.clone();
                let named_group = if count == 0 { enclosing_named_group.clone() } else { Some(builder.next_named_group()?) };
                let mut named_formals = Vec::new();
                let mut parameter_work = Vec::new();
                let self_binding = (count != 0).then(|| (data.spelling.clone(), data.id.clone()));
                // The temporary self identity belongs to the initializer scope;
                // parameters may shadow it, and Publish restores then installs it.
                if let Some((spelling, id)) = self_binding {
                    builder.push_binding(spelling, LocalSourceResolution::Local(id))?;
                }
                push(&mut builder.work, Work::Publish(binding, guard))?;
                for ordinal in 0..count {
                    let parameter = builder.bindings[binding as usize].parameters[ordinal].clone();
                    builder.parameter_work(&parameter, named_group.clone(), &mut parameter_work)?;
                    let next = builder.slot()?;
                    push(&mut named_formals, LocalSourceNamedFormal { parameter: parameter.id.clone(), lambda: index.clone() })?;
                    builder.set(
                        index,
                        &node,
                        scope,
                        named_group.clone(),
                        LocalSourceForm::Lambda {
                            parameter: parameter.clone(),
                            body: next.clone(),
                        },
                    )?;
                    index = next;
                    scope = LocalSourceScope::Parameter(parameter.id);
                }
                if count != 0 {
                    builder.record_named_group(named_group.clone().ok_or_else(invalid)?, LocalSourceNamedGroupOwner::Local(group_owner), &node,
                        enclosing_named_group, named_formals, index.clone())?;
                }
                push(
                    &mut builder.work,
                    Work::Expression(body(&node, HirVisibility::Private)?, index, scope, named_group.clone()),
                )?;
                for work in parameter_work.into_iter().rev() { push(&mut builder.work, work)?; }
            }
            Work::Publish(binding, guard) => {
                builder.restore(guard);
                let data = &builder.bindings[binding as usize];
                builder.push_binding(
                    data.spelling.clone(),
                    LocalSourceResolution::Local(data.id.clone()),
                )?;
            }
        }
    }
    let mut expressions = Vec::new();
    expressions
        .try_reserve_exact(builder.expressions.len())
        .map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
    for expression in builder.expressions {
        expressions.push(expression.ok_or_else(invalid)?);
    }
    let mut retained_root_parameters = Vec::new();
    retained_root_parameters.try_reserve_exact(root_parameters.len())
        .map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
    retained_root_parameters.extend(root_parameters.iter().cloned());
    let source = LocalSource {
        owner: owner.clone(),
        body: root,
        expressions,
        root_parameters: retained_root_parameters,
        member_header: member_header.cloned(),
        bindings: builder.bindings,
        where_clauses: builder.where_clauses,
        named_groups: builder.named_groups,
        annotation: if admitted.is_some() { source_annotation::form(&plan.node, owner, counters)? } else { None },
    };
    // Successful formation establishes that this actual root chain has the
    // same simple result without constructing a discarded associated tree.
    #[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
    let projection = if source.expressions().iter().any(|expr|
        matches!(&expr.form, LocalSourceForm::Lambda { parameter, .. } if parameter.record_pattern.is_some())) {
        // Ordinary HIR does not own these field binders. Its facade must not
        // re-resolve their uses outside the complete LocalSource lexical scope.
        SimpleBodyProjection::RecordPattern
    } else if source.expressions().iter().any(|expr| {
        matches!(expr.form, LocalSourceForm::Boolean(_) | LocalSourceForm::String(_) | LocalSourceForm::InterpolatedString { .. } | LocalSourceForm::If { .. })
    }) {
        SimpleBodyProjection::Unsupported
    } else {
        crate::direct_atom(&body_chain)
            .map_or(SimpleBodyProjection::Unsupported, SimpleBodyProjection::Atom)
    };
    #[cfg(not(any(feature = "shadow", test)))]
    let projection = SimpleBodyProjection::Unsupported;
    Ok((source, projection))
}
impl Builder<'_> {
    fn record_parameter_pattern(&mut self, node: &SyntaxNode, local: Option<&HirLocalId>)
        -> Result<Vec<LocalSourceRecordPatternField>, FormationError>
    {
        let mut fields = Vec::new();
        for field in node.children() {
            if field.kind() == SyntaxKind::RecordPatternSeparator { continue; }
            if field.kind() != SyntaxKind::RecordPatternField || has_recovery(&field) { return Err(unsupported()); }
            let mut parts = field.children_with_tokens().filter(|part| !matches!(part.kind(),
                SyntaxKind::Whitespace | SyntaxKind::Newline | SyntaxKind::LineComment | SyntaxKind::BlockComment));
            let label = parts.next().and_then(|part| part.into_token())
                .filter(|token| token.kind() == SyntaxKind::Identifier).ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
            let default = match parts.next() {
                None => None,
                Some(equals) if equals.kind() == SyntaxKind::Equals => {
                    let value = parts.next().and_then(|part| part.into_node())
                        .filter(|node| node.kind() == SyntaxKind::OperatorChain).ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                    let slot = self.slot()?;
                    self.default_nodes.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
                    self.default_nodes.insert(slot.clone(), value);
                    Some(slot)
                }
                _ => return Err(unsupported()),
            };
            if parts.next().is_some() { return Err(unsupported()); }
            let binder = self.parameter_id()?;
            let source = self.key(&field)?;
            let identity = self.counters.source_identity.as_mut().ok_or_else(invalid)?;
            identity.record_parameter(binder.clone(), source.clone());
            if let Some(local) = local { identity.local_parameter_owners.insert(binder.clone(), local.clone()); }
            let range = label.text_range();
            push(&mut fields, LocalSourceRecordPatternField {
                label: label.text().into(), source,
                range: usize::from(range.start())..usize::from(range.end()), binder, default,
            })?;
        }
        Ok(fields)
    }

    fn parameter_work(&mut self, parameter: &LocalSourceParameter, group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>, work: &mut Vec<Work>)
        -> Result<(), FormationError>
    {
        if let Some(fields) = &parameter.record_pattern {
            for field in fields {
                if let Some(default) = &field.default {
                    let node = self.default_nodes.remove(default).ok_or_else(invalid)?;
                    push(work, Work::Expression(node, default.clone(), LocalSourceScope::Parameter(parameter.id.clone()), group.clone()))?;
                }
                push(work, Work::BindParameter(field.binder.clone(), field.label.clone()))?;
            }
        } else if !parameter.unit_pattern {
            push(work, Work::BindParameter(parameter.id.clone(), parameter.spelling.clone()))?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn parameter_id(&mut self) -> Result<HirParameterId, FormationError> {
        let ordinal = self.next_parameter;
        self.next_parameter = ordinal.checked_add(1).ok_or(HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
        Ok(HirParameterId::new(self.owner.clone(), ordinal))
    }
    fn push_binding(
        &mut self,
        spelling: Box<str>,
        resolution: LocalSourceResolution,
    ) -> Result<(), FormationError> {
        let previous = self.latest.get(spelling.as_ref()).copied();
        self.lexical
            .try_reserve(1)
            .map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
        let new_key = if previous.is_none() {
            self.latest
                .try_reserve(1)
                .map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
            Some(spelling.clone())
        } else {
            None
        };
        let index = self.lexical.len();
        self.lexical.push(LexicalEntry {
            spelling,
            resolution,
            previous,
        });
        if let Some(key) = new_key {
            self.latest.insert(key, index);
        } else {
            *self
                .latest
                .get_mut(self.lexical[index].spelling.as_ref())
                .expect("active spelling has a latest binding") = index;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn restore(&mut self, depth: usize) {
        while self.lexical.len() > depth {
            let entry = self.lexical.pop().expect("binding above restore depth");
            if let Some(previous) = entry.previous {
                *self
                    .latest
                    .get_mut(entry.spelling.as_ref())
                    .expect("active spelling has a latest binding") = previous;
            } else {
                self.latest.remove(entry.spelling.as_ref());
            }
        }
    }
    fn slot(&mut self) -> Result<LocalSourceIndex, FormationError> {
        let index = u32::try_from(self.expressions.len())
            .map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
        push(&mut self.expressions, None)?;
        Ok(LocalSourceIndex {
            owner: self.owner.clone(),
            ordinal: index,
        })
    }
    fn key(&self, node: &SyntaxNode) -> Result<SourceNodeKey, FormationError> {
        self.counters
            .source_nodes
            .get(node)
            .cloned()
            .ok_or_else(invalid)
    }
    fn next_named_group(&self) -> Result<LocalSourceNamedGroupId, FormationError> {
        Ok(LocalSourceNamedGroupId { owner: self.owner.clone(), ordinal: u32::try_from(self.named_groups.len()).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)? })
    }
    fn record_named_group(&mut self, id: LocalSourceNamedGroupId, owner: LocalSourceNamedGroupOwner, node: &SyntaxNode,
        enclosing: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>, formals: Vec<LocalSourceNamedFormal>, terminal_body: LocalSourceIndex,
    ) -> Result<(), FormationError> {
        let terminal_lambda = formals.last().ok_or_else(invalid)?.lambda.clone();
        let source = self.key(node)?;
        push(&mut self.named_groups, LocalSourceNamedGroup { id, owner, source, enclosing, formals, terminal_lambda, terminal_body })
    }
    fn set(
        &mut self,
        index: LocalSourceIndex,
        node: &SyntaxNode,
        scope: LocalSourceScope,
        named_group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>,
        form: LocalSourceForm,
    ) -> Result<(), FormationError> {
        self.set_at_range(index, node, range_of(node), scope, named_group, form)
    }
    fn set_at_range(
        &mut self,
        index: LocalSourceIndex,
        node: &SyntaxNode,
        range: Range<usize>,
        scope: LocalSourceScope,
        named_group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>,
        form: LocalSourceForm,
    ) -> Result<(), FormationError> {
        let source = self.key(node)?;
        let occurrence = if index.ordinal == 0 {
            match self.owner {
                SourceRootId::Expression(root) => root.occurrence().clone(),
                SourceRootId::Definition(_) | SourceRootId::Member(_) => next_occurrence(self.artifact, self.next_occurrence)?,
            }
        } else { next_occurrence(self.artifact, self.next_occurrence)? };
        self.counters
            .source_identity
            .as_mut()
            .ok_or_else(invalid)?
            .record_occurrence(occurrence.clone(), source.clone());
        self.expressions[index.ordinal as usize] = Some(LocalSourceExpr {
            generated_string: None,
            occurrence,
            source,
            named_group,
            range,
            scope,
            form,
        });
        Ok(())
    }
    fn generated_string(
        &mut self, node: &SyntaxNode, range: Range<usize>, scope: LocalSourceScope,
        named_group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>, operation: LocalSourceStringOperation, form: LocalSourceForm,
    ) -> Result<LocalSourceIndex, FormationError> {
        let index = self.slot()?;
        self.set_at_range(index.clone(), node, range, scope, named_group, form)?;
        self.expressions[index.ordinal as usize].as_mut().ok_or_else(invalid)?.generated_string = Some(operation);
        Ok(index)
    }
    fn associated_operator_chain(
        &mut self,
        chain_node: SyntaxNode,
        associated: Arc<crate::HirExpr>,
        index: LocalSourceIndex,
        scope: LocalSourceScope,
        named_group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>,
        expected_operator_uses: usize,
    ) -> Result<(), FormationError> {
        let chain_range = range_of(&chain_node);
        let mut associated_nodes = Vec::new();
        collect_associated_range(&associated, &chain_range, &mut associated_nodes)?;
        let [associated_root] = associated_nodes.as_slice() else { return Err(unsupported()); };
        let mut pending = Vec::new();
        push(&mut pending, (*associated_root, index, true))?;
        let mut seen = std::collections::HashSet::<Range<usize>>::new();
        while let Some((expression, index, root)) = pending.pop() {
            match expression {
                crate::HirExpr::Apply { operator, operands, range } => {
                    let (left, right, use_kind) = match (operator.fixity(), operands.as_ref()) {
                        (crate::OperatorFixity::Infix, [left, right]) => (left, Some(right), SyntaxKind::InfixOperatorUse),
                        (crate::OperatorFixity::Prefix, [operand]) => (operand, None, SyntaxKind::PrefixOperatorUse),
                        (crate::OperatorFixity::Suffix, [operand]) => (operand, None, SyntaxKind::SuffixOperatorUse),
                        _ => return Err(unsupported()),
                    };
                    let source_range = operator.range().clone();
                    let mut operator_nodes = chain_node.descendants().filter(|node|
                        node.kind() == use_kind
                            && range_of(node) == source_range
                    );
                    let operator_node = operator_nodes.next().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                    if operator_nodes.next().is_some()
                        || self.counters.source_operator_uses.get(&source_range) != Some(operator)
                        || !seen.insert(source_range.clone())
                    {
                        return Err(unsupported());
                    }
                    let operator_source = self.key(&operator_node)?;
                    let left_index = self.slot()?;
                    let right_index = right.map(|_| self.slot()).transpose()?;
                    let source_node = if root { &chain_node } else { &operator_node };
                    let retained_range = if root { range_of(&chain_node) } else { range.clone() };
                    self.set_at_range(
                        index, source_node, retained_range, scope.clone(), named_group.clone(),
                        LocalSourceForm::OperatorApplication {
                            operator_source,
                            operator: operator.clone(),
                            left: left_index.clone(),
                            right: right_index.clone(),
                        },
                    )?;
                    for (operand, child_index) in right.zip(right_index).into_iter().chain(std::iter::once((left, left_index))) {
                        match operand {
                            crate::HirExpr::Apply { .. } => push(&mut pending, (operand, child_index, false))?,
                            crate::HirExpr::Value { .. } => self.associated_value(
                                operand, &chain_node, child_index, scope.clone(), named_group.clone(),
                            )?,
                            crate::HirExpr::Error { .. } => return Err(unsupported()),
                        }
                    }
                }
                crate::HirExpr::Value { .. } | crate::HirExpr::Error { .. } => {
                    return Err(unsupported());
                }
            }
        }
        if expected_operator_uses == 0 || seen.len() != expected_operator_uses {
            return Err(unsupported());
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn associated_value(
        &mut self,
        value: &crate::HirExpr,
        container: &SyntaxNode,
        index: LocalSourceIndex,
        scope: LocalSourceScope,
        named_group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>,
    ) -> Result<(), FormationError> {
        let mut value = value;
        let mut index = index;
        loop {
            let crate::HirExpr::Value { kind, range, children } = value else {
                return Err(unsupported());
            };
            if matches!(kind, SyntaxKind::MlArgument | SyntaxKind::CallTail) {
                let (callee, arguments) = children.split_first().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                let mut head = callee;
                while let crate::HirExpr::Value { kind: SyntaxKind::MlArgument | SyntaxKind::CallTail, children, .. } = head {
                    head = children.first().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                }
                if let crate::HirExpr::Value { kind: SyntaxKind::SymbolExpression, range: head_range, .. } = head {
                    // Recover only the fixed tails proven by this associated
                    // operand. Their arguments remain owned by the original
                    // chain; no source text is parsed a second time.
                    let mut nodes = container.descendants().filter(|node|
                        node.kind() == SyntaxKind::SymbolExpression && range_of(node) == *head_range);
                    let head_node = nodes.next().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                    if nodes.next().is_some() { return Err(unsupported()); }
                    let mut cursor = value;
                    let mut prefix = Vec::new();
                    while let crate::HirExpr::Value { kind: kind @ (SyntaxKind::MlArgument | SyntaxKind::CallTail), range, children } = cursor {
                        let (callee, arguments) = children.split_first().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                        let mut tails = container.descendants().filter(|node|
                            node.kind() == *kind
                                && range_of(node).end == range.end && range_of(node).start >= callee.range().end);
                        let tail = tails.next().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                        if tails.next().is_some() || tail.children().count() != arguments.len()
                            || tail.children().zip(arguments).any(|(node, arg)| range_of(&node) != *arg.range())
                        { return Err(unsupported()); }
                        push(&mut prefix, tail)?;
                        cursor = callee;
                    }
                    prefix.reverse();
                    return self.variant(&head_node, &prefix, index, scope, named_group);
                }
                let mut tails = container.descendants().filter(|node| {
                    node.kind() == *kind
                        && range_of(node).end == range.end
                        && range_of(node).start >= callee.range().end
                });
                let tail = tails.next().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                if tails.next().is_some() || has_recovery(&tail)
                    || tail.children_with_tokens().any(|element| matches!(
                        element.kind(), SyntaxKind::Comma | SyntaxKind::ExpressionDelimitedSeparator,
                    ))
                {
                    return Err(unsupported());
                }
                let argument_node = match arguments {
                    [] if *kind == SyntaxKind::CallTail && tail.children().next().is_none() => None,
                    [argument] => {
                        let node = only_child(&tail)?;
                        if range_of(&node) != *argument.range() { return Err(unsupported()); }
