                        Some(node)
                    }
                    _ => return Err(unsupported()),
                };
                let callee_index = self.slot()?;
                let argument_index = self.slot()?;
                self.set_at_range(
                    index, &tail, range.clone(), scope.clone(), named_group.clone(),
                    LocalSourceForm::Apply {
                        callee: callee_index.clone(), argument: argument_index.clone(), source_form: *kind,
                    },
                )?;
                if let Some(node) = argument_node {
                    push(&mut self.work, Work::Expression(node, argument_index, scope.clone(), named_group.clone()))?;
                } else {
                    self.set(argument_index, &tail, scope.clone(), named_group.clone(), LocalSourceForm::Unit)?;
                }
                value = callee;
                index = callee_index;
                continue;
            }
            if *kind != SyntaxKind::FieldTail {
                let mut nodes = container.descendants().filter(|node|
                    node.kind() == *kind && range_of(node) == *range
                );
                let node = nodes.next().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                if nodes.next().is_some() || node == *container {
                    return Err(unsupported());
                }
                return push(&mut self.work, Work::Expression(node, index, scope, named_group));
            }
            let [receiver] = children.as_slice() else { return Err(unsupported()); };
            let mut tails = container.descendants().filter(|node| {
                node.kind() == SyntaxKind::FieldTail
                    && range_of(node).end == range.end
                    && range_of(node).start >= range.start
            });
            let tail = tails.next().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
            if tails.next().is_some() || has_recovery(&tail) {
                return Err(unsupported());
            }
            let tokens: Vec<_> = tail.children_with_tokens().filter(|element| !matches!(
                element.kind(), SyntaxKind::Whitespace | SyntaxKind::Newline
                    | SyntaxKind::LineComment | SyntaxKind::BlockComment,
            )).collect();
            let [dot, member] = tokens.as_slice() else { return Err(unsupported()); };
            let dot = dot.as_token().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
            let member = member.as_token().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
            if dot.kind() != SyntaxKind::Dot || member.kind() != SyntaxKind::Identifier {
                return Err(unsupported());
            }
            let receiver_index = self.slot()?;
            self.set_at_range(
                index, &tail, range.clone(), scope.clone(), named_group.clone(),
                LocalSourceForm::FieldTail {
                    receiver: receiver_index.clone(),
                    member: member.text().into(),
                    member_range: crate::range_of_token(&member),
                },
            )?;
            value = receiver;
            index = receiver_index;
        }
    }
    fn variant(
        &mut self, head: &SyntaxNode, tails: &[SyntaxNode], index: LocalSourceIndex,
        scope: LocalSourceScope, named_group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>,
    ) -> Result<(), FormationError> {
        let (tag, tag_range) = symbol_tag(head)?;
        let mut payloads = Vec::new();
        let mut children = Vec::new();
        for tail in tails {
            if has_recovery(tail) { return Err(unsupported()); }
            let arguments: Vec<_> = tail.children().collect();
            if arguments.iter().any(|node| node.kind() != SyntaxKind::OperatorChain)
                || (tail.kind() == SyntaxKind::MlArgument && arguments.len() != 1)
                || !matches!(tail.kind(), SyntaxKind::MlArgument | SyntaxKind::CallTail)
            { return Err(unsupported()); }
            if arguments.is_empty() {
                let unit = self.slot()?;
                self.set(unit.clone(), tail, scope.clone(), named_group.clone(), LocalSourceForm::Unit)?;
                push(&mut payloads, unit)?;
            }
            for argument in arguments {
                let slot = self.slot()?;
                push(&mut payloads, slot.clone())?;
                push(&mut children, (argument, slot))?;
            }
        }
        let range = range_of(head).start..tails.last().map_or(range_of(head).end, |tail| range_of(tail).end);
        self.set_at_range(index, head, range, scope.clone(), named_group.clone(),
            LocalSourceForm::Variant { tag, tag_range, payloads })?;
        for (node, index) in children.into_iter().rev() {
            push(&mut self.work, Work::Expression(node, index, scope.clone(), named_group.clone()))?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn interpolated_string(
        &mut self, node: SyntaxNode, index: LocalSourceIndex,
        scope: LocalSourceScope, named_group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>,
    ) -> Result<(), FormationError> {
        let (spelling, literal_range) = string_literal_source(&node)?;
        let mut parts = Vec::new();
        let mut work = Vec::new();
        for part in node.children().filter(|child| child.kind() == SyntaxKind::StringInterpolation) {
            if has_recovery(&part) { return Err(unsupported()); }
            let mut body = part.children().filter(|child| child.kind() == SyntaxKind::StringInterpolationBody);
            let body = body.next().filter(|_| body.next().is_none()).ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
            let mut format = None;
            let mut open = None;
            let mut percent = None;
            let mut close = None;
            for token in part.children_with_tokens().filter_map(|element| element.into_token()) {
                match token.kind() {
                    SyntaxKind::StringInterpolationFormatText if format.is_none() => format = Some(token),
                    SyntaxKind::StringInterpolationPercent if percent.is_none() => percent = Some(token),
                    SyntaxKind::StringInterpolationOpenBrace if open.is_none() => open = Some(token),
                    SyntaxKind::StringInterpolationCloseBrace if close.is_none() => close = Some(token),
                    SyntaxKind::Whitespace | SyntaxKind::Newline | SyntaxKind::LineComment | SyntaxKind::BlockComment => {},
                    _ => return Err(unsupported()),
                }
            }
            let (percent, open, close) = (percent.ok_or_else(unsupported)?, open.ok_or_else(unsupported)?, close.ok_or_else(unsupported)?);
            let range = crate::range_of_token(&percent).start..crate::range_of_token(&close).end;
            let format_range = crate::range_of_token(&percent).end..crate::range_of_token(&open).start;
            let format = format.map_or_else(|| Box::<str>::from(""), |token| token.text().into());
            if format.len() != format_range.len() || range.start < literal_range.start || range.end > literal_range.end {
                return Err(unsupported());
            }
            let body_index = self.slot()?;
            let format_spec = string::parse_format(&format)?;
            push(&mut parts, LocalSourceInterpolation { source: self.key(&part)?, range, format, format_spec, format_range, body: body_index.clone() })?;
            push(&mut work, (body, body_index))?;
        }
        if parts.is_empty() { return Err(unsupported()); }
        let pieces = string::pieces(&node, &parts)?;
        let lowered = self.lower_string_plan(&node, &literal_range, &parts, &pieces, &scope, &named_group)?;
        self.set(index, &node, scope.clone(), named_group.clone(), LocalSourceForm::InterpolatedString { spelling, literal_range, parts, pieces, lowered })?;
        for (body, index) in work.into_iter().rev() {
            push(&mut self.work, Work::Expression(body, index, scope.clone(), named_group.clone()))?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn lower_string_plan(
        &mut self, node: &SyntaxNode, literal_range: &Range<usize>, parts: &[LocalSourceInterpolation],
        pieces: &[LocalSourceStringPiece], scope: &LocalSourceScope, named_group: &Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>,
    ) -> Result<Option<LocalSourceIndex>, FormationError> {
        if parts.iter().any(|part| part.format_spec.has_layout()) { return Ok(None); }
        let concat = self.counters.string_concat.clone();
        if pieces.len() > 1 && concat.is_none() { return Ok(None); }
        let mut interpolation_nodes = node.children().filter(|child| child.kind() == SyntaxKind::StringInterpolation);
        let mut result = None;
        for (ordinal, piece) in pieces.iter().enumerate() {
            let ordinal = u32::try_from(ordinal).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
            let value = match piece {
                LocalSourceStringPiece::Text { range, value } => self.generated_string(
                    node, range.clone(), scope.clone(), named_group.clone(),
                    LocalSourceStringOperation::Text { piece: ordinal }, LocalSourceForm::DecodedString(value.clone()),
                )?,
                LocalSourceStringPiece::Interpolation(part) => {
                    let part = parts.get(*part as usize).ok_or_else(invalid)?;
                    let part_node = interpolation_nodes.next().ok_or_else(invalid)?;
                    if self.key(&part_node)? != part.source { return Err(invalid()); }
                    let LocalSourceFormatOperation::Member(member) = part.format_spec.operation() else { return Err(invalid()); };
                    self.generated_string(&part_node, part.range.clone(), scope.clone(), named_group.clone(),
                        LocalSourceStringOperation::FormatSelection { piece: ordinal },
                        LocalSourceForm::GeneratedFormatSelection { receiver: part.body.clone(), member: member.into() })?
                }
            };
            result = Some(if let Some(left) = result {
                let provider = concat.as_ref().ok_or_else(invalid)?;
                let reference = self.generated_string(node, literal_range.clone(), scope.clone(), named_group.clone(),
                    LocalSourceStringOperation::ConcatReference { piece: ordinal },
                    LocalSourceForm::Name { spelling: "std::text::str::concat".into(),
                        resolution: LocalSourceResolution::Imported(provider.clone()) })?;
                let partial = self.generated_string(node, literal_range.clone(), scope.clone(), named_group.clone(),
                    LocalSourceStringOperation::ConcatFirstArgument { piece: ordinal },
                    LocalSourceForm::Apply { callee: reference, argument: left, source_form: SyntaxKind::StringLiteral })?;
                self.generated_string(node, literal_range.clone(), scope.clone(), named_group.clone(),
                    LocalSourceStringOperation::ConcatSecondArgument { piece: ordinal },
                    LocalSourceForm::Apply { callee: partial, argument: value, source_form: SyntaxKind::StringLiteral })?
            } else { value });
        }
        if interpolation_nodes.next().is_some() { return Err(invalid()); }
        Ok(result)
    }
    fn expression(
        &mut self,
        node: SyntaxNode,
        index: LocalSourceIndex,
        scope: LocalSourceScope,
        named_group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>,
    ) -> Result<(), FormationError> {
        match node.kind() {
            SyntaxKind::OperatorChain => {
                let mut children = Vec::new();
                for child in node.children() {
                    push(&mut children, child)?;
                }
                let (head, all_tails) = children.split_first().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                let operator_uses = children.iter().enumerate()
                    .filter(|(_, node)| matches!(node.kind(), SyntaxKind::InfixOperatorUse | SyntaxKind::PrefixOperatorUse | SyntaxKind::SuffixOperatorUse))
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>();
                if !operator_uses.is_empty() {
                    let source_range = range_of(operator_uses[0].1);
                    let associated = self.counters.source_associated_chains
                        .get(&source_range).cloned().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                    self.associated_operator_chain(
                        node, associated, index, scope, named_group, operator_uses.len(),
                    )?;
                    return Ok(());
                }
                let variant_count = if head.kind() == SyntaxKind::SymbolExpression {
                    all_tails.iter().take_while(|tail| matches!(tail.kind(), SyntaxKind::MlArgument | SyntaxKind::CallTail)).count()
                } else { 0 };
                let (variant_tails, all_tails) = all_tails.split_at(variant_count);
                let path_tail_count = all_tails
                    .iter()
                    .take_while(|tail| tail.kind() == SyntaxKind::PathTail)
                    .count();
                let path_tails = &all_tails[..path_tail_count];
                let tails = &all_tails[path_tail_count..];
                let mut current = index;
                for tail in tails.iter().rev() {
                    if tail.kind() == SyntaxKind::TypeAnnotationTail {
                        let annotation = source_annotation::parse_expression_ascription(
                            tail,
                            self.owner,
                            self.counters,
                        )?;
                        let inner = self.slot()?;
                        self.set(
                            current,
                            tail,
                            scope.clone(),
                            named_group.clone(),
                            LocalSourceForm::Ascription {
                                inner: inner.clone(),
                                annotation,
                            },
                        )?;
                        current = inner;
                        continue;
                    }
                    if tail.kind() == SyntaxKind::FieldTail {
                        if has_recovery(tail) {
                            return Err(unsupported());
                        }
                        let tokens: Vec<_> = tail
                            .children_with_tokens()
                            .filter(|element| {
                                !matches!(
                                    element.kind(),
                                    SyntaxKind::Whitespace
                                        | SyntaxKind::Newline
                                        | SyntaxKind::LineComment
                                        | SyntaxKind::BlockComment
                                )
                            })
                            .collect();
                        let [dot, member] = tokens.as_slice() else {
                            return Err(unsupported());
                        };
                        let dot = dot.as_token().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                        let member = member.as_token().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                        if dot.kind() != SyntaxKind::Dot
                            || member.kind() != SyntaxKind::Identifier
                        {
                            return Err(unsupported());
                        }
                        let receiver = self.slot()?;
                        self.set(
                            current,
                            tail,
                            scope.clone(),
                            named_group.clone(),
                            LocalSourceForm::FieldTail {
                                receiver: receiver.clone(),
                                member: member.text().into(),
                                member_range: crate::range_of_token(&member),
                            },
                        )?;
                        current = receiver;
                        continue;
                    }
                    if !matches!(
                        tail.kind(),
                        SyntaxKind::MlArgument
                            | SyntaxKind::CallTail
                            | SyntaxKind::ColonApplicationTail
                    ) {
                        return Err(unsupported());
                    }
                    if tail.children_with_tokens().any(|element| {
                        matches!(
                            element.kind(),
                            SyntaxKind::Comma | SyntaxKind::ExpressionDelimitedSeparator
                        )
                    }) {
                        return Err(unsupported());
                    }
                    let argument_node = if tail.kind() == SyntaxKind::CallTail
                        && tail.children().next().is_none()
                    {
                        None
                    } else {
                        Some(if tail.kind() == SyntaxKind::ColonApplicationTail {
                            inline_colon_argument(tail)?
                        } else {
                            only_child(tail)?
                        })
                    };
                    let callee = self.slot()?;
                    let argument = self.slot()?;
                    self.set(
                        current,
                        tail,
                        scope.clone(),
                        named_group.clone(),
                        LocalSourceForm::Apply {
                            callee: callee.clone(),
                            argument: argument.clone(),
                            source_form: tail.kind(),
                        },
                    )?;
                    if let Some(argument_node) = argument_node {
                        push(
                            &mut self.work,
                            Work::Expression(argument_node, argument, scope.clone(), named_group.clone()),
                        )?;
                    } else {
                        self.set(argument, tail, scope.clone(), named_group.clone(), LocalSourceForm::Unit)?;
                    }
                    current = callee;
                }
                if head.kind() == SyntaxKind::SymbolExpression {
                    if !path_tails.is_empty() { return Err(unsupported()); }
                    self.variant(head, variant_tails, current, scope, named_group)?;
                } else if !path_tails.is_empty() {
                    if head.kind() != SyntaxKind::IdentifierExpression {
                        return Err(unsupported());
                    }
                    let mut path = vec![identifier_spelling(head)?];
                    for tail in path_tails {
                        if has_recovery(tail) { return Err(unsupported()); }
                        let tokens = tail.children_with_tokens().filter(|element| !matches!(
                            element.kind(), SyntaxKind::Whitespace | SyntaxKind::Newline
                                | SyntaxKind::LineComment | SyntaxKind::BlockComment,
                        )).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                        let [separator, segment] = tokens.as_slice() else { return Err(unsupported()); };
                        let separator = separator.as_token().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                        let segment = segment.as_token().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                        if separator.kind() != SyntaxKind::ColonColon || segment.kind() != SyntaxKind::Identifier {
                            return Err(unsupported());
                        }
                        path.push(segment.text().into());
                    }
                    let qualified = path.iter().map(AsRef::as_ref).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("::");
                    #[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
                    if let Some(super::ModuleNameResolution::ImportedOperation(operation)) = self.namespace.get(&qualified) {
                        self.set(current, &node, scope, named_group.clone(), LocalSourceForm::Operation {
                            resolution: source_annotation::SourceOperationResolution::Resolved(
                                operation.operation.clone(),
                            ),
                        })?;
                        return Ok(());
                    }
                    if let Some(super::ModuleNameResolution::Imported(id)) = self.namespace.get(&qualified) {
                        self.set(current, &node, scope, named_group.clone(), LocalSourceForm::Name {
                            spelling: qualified.into(),
                            resolution: LocalSourceResolution::Imported(id.clone()),
                        })?;
                    } else if path_tails.len() == 1 {
                        let form = match source_annotation::pinned_primitive_op(path[0].as_ref(), &path[1]) {
                            Some(operation) => LocalSourceForm::Primitive { operation },
                            None => LocalSourceForm::Operation {
                                resolution: self.resolve_operation(path[0].as_ref(), path[1].clone()),
                            },
                        };
                        self.set(current, &path_tails[0], scope, named_group.clone(), form)?;
                    } else {
                        // Preserve a valid qualified name when this projection
                        // has no import receipt. Candidate admission still
                        // rejects Unresolved; no module/operation is invented.
                        self.set(current, &node, scope, named_group.clone(), LocalSourceForm::Name {
                            spelling: qualified.into(),
                            resolution: LocalSourceResolution::Unresolved,
                        })?;
                    }
                } else {
                    push(
                        &mut self.work,
                        Work::Expression(head.clone(), current, scope, named_group.clone()),
                    )?;
                }
            }
            SyntaxKind::SymbolExpression => self.variant(&node, &[], index, scope, named_group)?,
            SyntaxKind::LambdaExpression => {
                let mut elements = node.children_with_tokens().filter(|element| !matches!(
                    element.kind(), SyntaxKind::Whitespace | SyntaxKind::Newline
                        | SyntaxKind::LineComment | SyntaxKind::BlockComment,
                ));
                let slash = elements.next().and_then(|element| element.into_token()).ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                let binder = elements.next().and_then(|element| element.into_token()).ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                let arrow = elements.next().and_then(|element| element.into_token()).ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                let body_node = elements.next().and_then(|element| element.into_node()).ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                if slash.kind() != SyntaxKind::Backslash || binder.kind() != SyntaxKind::Identifier
                    || arrow.kind() != SyntaxKind::Arrow || body_node.kind() != SyntaxKind::OperatorChain
                    || elements.next().is_some()
                {
                    return Err(unsupported());
                }
                let id = self.parameter_id()?;
                let source = self.key(&node)?;
                let range = crate::range_of_token(&binder);
                self.counters.source_identity.as_mut().ok_or_else(invalid)?
                    .record_inline_parameter(id.clone(), source.clone(), range.clone());
                let parameter = LocalSourceParameter {
                    id: id.clone(), source, range, spelling: binder.text().into(),
                    scope: scope.clone(), annotation: None,
                    unit_pattern: false,
                    record_pattern: None,
                };
                let body = self.slot()?;
                push(&mut self.work, Work::Restore(self.lexical.len()))?;
                self.push_binding(parameter.spelling.clone(), LocalSourceResolution::Parameter(id.clone()))?;
                push(&mut self.work, Work::Expression(body_node, body.clone(), LocalSourceScope::Parameter(id), named_group.clone()))?;
                self.set(index, &node, scope, named_group, LocalSourceForm::Lambda { parameter, body })?;
            }
            SyntaxKind::IdentifierExpression => {
                let spelling = identifier_spelling(&node)?;
                if !self.latest.contains_key(spelling.as_ref()) {
                    let boolean = match spelling.as_ref() {
                        "true" => Some(true),
                        "false" => Some(false),
                        _ => None,
                    };
                    if let Some(value) = boolean {
                        self.set(index, &node, scope, named_group.clone(), LocalSourceForm::Boolean(value))?;
                        return Ok(());
                    }
                }
                let resolution = self
                    .latest
                    .get(spelling.as_ref())
                    .map(|&index| self.lexical[index].resolution.clone())
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| match self.namespace.get(spelling.as_ref()) {
                        Some(super::ModuleNameResolution::Local(id)) => {
                            LocalSourceResolution::ModuleDef(id.clone())
                        }
                        Some(super::ModuleNameResolution::Imported(id)) => {
                            LocalSourceResolution::Imported(id.clone())
                        }
                        #[cfg(any(feature = "shadow", test))]
                        Some(super::ModuleNameResolution::ImportedOperation(_)) => LocalSourceResolution::Unresolved,
                        Some(super::ModuleNameResolution::Ambiguous) => LocalSourceResolution::Ambiguous,
                        None => LocalSourceResolution::Unresolved,
                    });
                self.set(
                    index,
                    &node,
                    scope,
                    named_group.clone(),
                    LocalSourceForm::Name {
                        spelling,
                        resolution,
                    },
                )?;
            }
            SyntaxKind::IntegerLiteral => self.set(
                index,
                &node,
                scope,
                named_group.clone(),
                LocalSourceForm::Integer(integer_spelling(&node)?),
            )?,
            SyntaxKind::StringLiteral => {
                if node.children().any(|child| child.kind() == SyntaxKind::StringInterpolation) {
                    self.interpolated_string(node, index, scope, named_group)?;
                } else {
                    self.set(index, &node, scope, named_group.clone(), LocalSourceForm::String(string_spelling(&node)?))?;
                }
            }
            SyntaxKind::StringInterpolationBody => self.block(node, index, scope, named_group)?,
            SyntaxKind::CatchExpression => self.catch(node, index, scope, named_group.clone())?,
            SyntaxKind::CaseExpression => self.case(node, index, scope, named_group.clone())?,
            SyntaxKind::IfExpression => self.conditional(node, index, scope, named_group.clone())?,
            SyntaxKind::ParenthesizedExpression => {
                let mut children = Vec::new();
                let mut has_comma = false;
                for element in node.children_with_tokens() {
                    match element.kind() {
                        SyntaxKind::Comma => has_comma = true,
                        SyntaxKind::Whitespace
                        | SyntaxKind::Newline
                        | SyntaxKind::LineComment
                        | SyntaxKind::BlockComment
                        | SyntaxKind::LParen
                        | SyntaxKind::RParen => {}
                        SyntaxKind::ExpressionDelimitedSeparator => return Err(unsupported()),
                        _ => {
                            let Some(child) = element.into_node() else {
                                return Err(unsupported());
                            };
                            push(&mut children, child)?;
                        }
                    }
                }
                if children.is_empty() {
                    self.set(
                        index,
                        &node,
                        scope,
                        named_group.clone(),
                        LocalSourceForm::Unit,
                    )?;
                    return Ok(());
                }
                if children.len() == 1 && !has_comma {
                    let child = children.pop().ok_or_else(invalid)?;
                    let inner = self.slot()?;
                    self.set(
                        index,
                        &node,
                        scope.clone(),
                        named_group.clone(),
                        LocalSourceForm::Group {
                            inner: inner.clone(),
                        },
                    )?;
                    push(
                        &mut self.work,
                        Work::Expression(child, inner, scope, named_group.clone()),
                    )?;
                } else {
                    let mut elements = Vec::new();
                    elements
                        .try_reserve_exact(children.len())
                        .map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
                    let mut slots = Vec::new();
                    slots
                        .try_reserve_exact(children.len())
                        .map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
                    for _ in 0..children.len() {
                        let slot = self.slot()?;
                        elements.push(slot.clone());
                        slots.push(slot);
                    }
                    self.set(
                        index,
                        &node,
                        scope.clone(),
                        named_group.clone(),
                        LocalSourceForm::Tuple { elements },
                    )?;
                    for (child, slot) in children.into_iter().zip(slots).rev() {
                        push(
                            &mut self.work,
                            Work::Expression(child, slot, scope.clone(), named_group.clone()),
                        )?;
                    }
                }
            }
            SyntaxKind::BracedStatementBlockExpression => {
                let Some(children) = record_fields(&node)? else {
                    return self.block(node, index, scope, named_group);
                };
                let mut fields = Vec::new();
                let mut work = Vec::new();
                for (label, child) in children {
                    let value = self.slot()?;
                    push(&mut fields, LocalSourceRecordField {
                        label: identifier_spelling(&label)?,
                        source: self.key(&label)?,
                        range: range_of(&label),
                        value: value.clone(),
                    })?;
                    push(&mut work, Work::Expression(child, value, scope.clone(), named_group.clone()))?;
                }
                self.set(index, &node, scope, named_group, LocalSourceForm::Record { fields })?;
                for child in work.into_iter().rev() { push(&mut self.work, child)?; }
            }
            SyntaxKind::IndentedStatementBlock => self.block(node, index, scope, named_group.clone())?,
            _ => return Err(unsupported()),
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn resolve_operation(&self, family: &str, member: Box<str>) -> source_annotation::SourceOperationResolution {
        use source_annotation::SourceOperationResolution;
        match self.counters.effect_namespace.get(family) {
            None => SourceOperationResolution::Unresolved,
            Some(families) if families.len() != 1 => SourceOperationResolution::Ambiguous,
            Some(families) => match self.counters.operation_namespace.get(&(families[0].clone(), member)) {
                None => SourceOperationResolution::Unresolved,
                Some(members) if members.len() != 1 => SourceOperationResolution::Ambiguous,
                Some(members) if members[0].visibility == HirVisibility::Private => SourceOperationResolution::Private,
                Some(members) => SourceOperationResolution::Resolved(members[0].clone()),
            },
        }
    }
    fn catch_value_pattern(
        &self, pattern: &SyntaxNode, arm_source: &SourceNodeKey, ordinal: u32,
        binders: &mut Vec<LocalSourceCatchBinder>,
    ) -> Result<LocalSourceCatchValuePattern, FormationError> {
        if pattern.kind() != SyntaxKind::Pattern { return Err(unsupported()); }
        let pattern = only_child(pattern)?;
        if pattern.kind() != SyntaxKind::IdentifierPattern { return Err(unsupported()); }
        let spelling = identifier_spelling(&pattern)?;
        if spelling.as_ref() == "_" { return Ok(LocalSourceCatchValuePattern::Wildcard); }
        let binder = LocalSourceCatchBinder {
            id: LocalSourceCatchBinderId { owner: self.owner.clone(), arm_source: arm_source.clone(), ordinal },
            source: self.key(&pattern)?, range: range_of(&pattern), spelling,
        };
        push(binders, binder.clone())?;
        Ok(LocalSourceCatchValuePattern::Binder(binder))
    }
    fn catch(
        &mut self,
        node: SyntaxNode,
        index: LocalSourceIndex,
        scope: LocalSourceScope,
        named_group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>,
    ) -> Result<(), FormationError> {
        let parts: Vec<_> = node.children().collect();
        let [scrutinee, block] = parts.as_slice() else { return Err(unsupported()); };
        if scrutinee.kind() != SyntaxKind::CatchScrutinee || block.kind() != SyntaxKind::CatchBlock {
            return Err(unsupported());
        }
        let scrutinee_node = only_child(scrutinee)?;
        let scrutinee_index = self.slot()?;
        let mut arms = Vec::new();
        let mut children = Vec::new();
        for arm in block.children() {
            if arm.kind() == SyntaxKind::CatchArmSeparator { continue; }
            if arm.kind() != SyntaxKind::CatchArm { return Err(unsupported()); }
            let mut parts = Vec::new();
            for child in arm.children() { push(&mut parts, child)?; }
            let (body, patterns) = parts.split_last().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
            if !matches!(body.kind(), SyntaxKind::OperatorChain | SyntaxKind::IndentedStatementBlock) { return Err(unsupported()); }
            let arm_source = self.key(&arm)?;
            let mut binders = Vec::new();
            let pattern = match patterns {
                [value] => LocalSourceCatchPattern::Value(self.catch_value_pattern(value, &arm_source, 0, &mut binders)?),
                [operation, continuation] => {
                    if operation.kind() != SyntaxKind::Pattern { return Err(unsupported()); }
                    let mut operation_parts = Vec::new();
                    for child in operation.children() { push(&mut operation_parts, child)?; }
                    let [head, path, call] = operation_parts.as_slice() else { return Err(unsupported()); };
                    if head.kind() != SyntaxKind::IdentifierPattern || path.kind() != SyntaxKind::PatternPathTail || call.kind() != SyntaxKind::PatternCallTail { return Err(unsupported()); }
                    let family = identifier_spelling(head)?;
                    let mut path_tokens = path.children_with_tokens().filter(|element| !matches!(element.kind(), SyntaxKind::Whitespace | SyntaxKind::Newline | SyntaxKind::LineComment | SyntaxKind::BlockComment));
                    if path_tokens.next().map(|token| token.kind()) != Some(SyntaxKind::ColonColon) { return Err(unsupported()); }
                    let member = path_tokens.next().and_then(|element| element.into_token()).ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                    if member.kind() != SyntaxKind::Identifier || path_tokens.next().is_some() { return Err(unsupported()); }
                    let resolution = self.resolve_operation(family.as_ref(), member.text().into());
                    let mut payloads = Vec::new();
                    let mut payload_nodes = call.children();
                    match (payload_nodes.next(), payload_nodes.next()) {
                        (None, None) => push(&mut payloads, LocalSourceCatchPayloadPattern::Unit { source: self.key(call)?, range: range_of(call) })?,
                        (Some(payload), None) => push(&mut payloads, LocalSourceCatchPayloadPattern::Value(self.catch_value_pattern(&payload, &arm_source, 0, &mut binders)?))?,
                        _ => return Err(unsupported()),
                    }
                    let continuation = self.catch_value_pattern(continuation, &arm_source, 1, &mut binders)?;
                    LocalSourceCatchPattern::Operation { resolution, operation_source: self.key(operation)?, payloads, continuation }
                }
                _ => return Err(unsupported()),
            };
            let body_index = self.slot()?;
            push(&mut arms, LocalSourceCatchArm { source: arm_source.clone(), range: range_of(&arm), pattern, body: body_index.clone() })?;
            push(&mut children, (body.clone(), body_index, LocalSourceScope::CatchArm { catch: index.clone(), arm: arm_source }, binders))?;

        }
        if arms.is_empty() { return Err(unsupported()); }
        self.set(index, &node, scope.clone(), named_group.clone(), LocalSourceForm::Catch { scrutinee: scrutinee_index.clone(), arms })?;
        for (body, index, scope, binder) in children.into_iter().rev() {
            push(&mut self.work, Work::CatchArm(body, index, scope, binder, named_group.clone()))?;
        }
        push(&mut self.work, Work::Restore(self.lexical.len()))?;
        push(&mut self.work, Work::Expression(scrutinee_node, scrutinee_index, scope, named_group.clone()))?;
        Ok(())
    }
    fn case_pattern_element(
        &self,
        pattern: &SyntaxNode,
        arm_source: &SourceNodeKey,
        next_binder: &mut u32,
        binders: &mut Vec<LocalSourceCatchBinder>,
    ) -> Result<LocalSourceCasePatternElement, FormationError> {
        if pattern.kind() != SyntaxKind::IdentifierPattern || has_recovery(pattern) {
            return Err(unsupported());
        }
        let spelling = identifier_spelling(pattern)?;
        if spelling.as_ref() == "_" {
            return Ok(LocalSourceCasePatternElement::Wildcard);
        }
        let ordinal = *next_binder;
        *next_binder = ordinal.checked_add(1).ok_or_else(invalid)?;
        let binder = LocalSourceCatchBinder {
            id: LocalSourceCatchBinderId {
                owner: self.owner.clone(),
                arm_source: arm_source.clone(),
                ordinal,
            },
            source: self.key(pattern)?,
            range: range_of(pattern),
            spelling,
        };
        push(binders, binder.clone())?;
        Ok(LocalSourceCasePatternElement::Binder(binder))
    }

    fn case_pattern(
        &mut self,
        pattern: &SyntaxNode,
        arm_source: &SourceNodeKey,
        scope: &LocalSourceScope,
        named_group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>,
        next_binder: &mut u32,
        binders: &mut Vec<LocalSourceCatchBinder>,
    ) -> Result<LocalSourceCasePattern, FormationError> {
        if has_recovery(pattern) { return Err(unsupported()); }
        match pattern.kind() {
            SyntaxKind::Pattern => {
                let children: Vec<_> = pattern.children().collect();
                if children.first().is_some_and(|head| head.kind() == SyntaxKind::SymbolPattern) {
                    let (head, tails) = children.split_first().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
                    let (tag, _) = symbol_tag(head)?;
                    let mut elements = Vec::new();
                    let mut names = std::collections::HashSet::new();
                    for tail in tails {
                        if !matches!(tail.kind(), SyntaxKind::PatternMlApplicationTail | SyntaxKind::PatternCallTail)
                            || has_recovery(tail) { return Err(unsupported()); }
                        let payloads: Vec<_> = tail.children().collect();
                        if payloads.is_empty() || (tail.kind() == SyntaxKind::PatternMlApplicationTail && payloads.len() != 1) {
                            return Err(unsupported());
                        }
                        for payload in payloads {
                            if payload.kind() != SyntaxKind::Pattern { return Err(unsupported()); }
                            let payload = only_child(&payload)?;
                            let element = self.case_pattern_element(&payload, arm_source, next_binder, binders)?;
                            if let LocalSourceCasePatternElement::Binder(binder) = &element {
                                names.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
                                if !names.insert(binder.spelling.clone()) { return Err(unsupported()); }
                            }
                            push(&mut elements, element)?;
                        }
                    }
                    Ok(LocalSourceCasePattern::Variant {
                        tag, source: self.key(pattern)?, range: range_of(pattern), elements,
                    })
                } else {
                    self.case_pattern(&only_child(pattern)?, arm_source, scope, named_group, next_binder, binders)
                }
            }
            SyntaxKind::IntegerPattern => {
                let pattern_index = self.slot()?;
                self.set(
                    pattern_index.clone(), pattern, scope.clone(), named_group,
                    LocalSourceForm::Integer(integer_spelling(pattern)?),
                )?;
                Ok(LocalSourceCasePattern::Integer(pattern_index))
            }
            SyntaxKind::IdentifierPattern => {
                let spelling = identifier_spelling(pattern)?;
                if spelling.as_ref() == "_" {
                    return Ok(LocalSourceCasePattern::Wildcard);
                }
                let ordinal = *next_binder;
                *next_binder = ordinal.checked_add(1).ok_or_else(invalid)?;
                let binder = LocalSourceCatchBinder {
                    id: LocalSourceCatchBinderId {
                        owner: self.owner.clone(), arm_source: arm_source.clone(), ordinal,
                    },
                    source: self.key(pattern)?, range: range_of(pattern), spelling,
                };
                push(binders, binder.clone())?;
                Ok(LocalSourceCasePattern::Binder(binder))
            }
            SyntaxKind::ParenthesizedPattern => {
                let mut children = Vec::new();
                let mut has_comma = false;
                for element in pattern.children_with_tokens() {
                    match element.kind() {
                        SyntaxKind::Comma => has_comma = true,
                        SyntaxKind::Whitespace | SyntaxKind::Newline
                        | SyntaxKind::LineComment | SyntaxKind::BlockComment
                        | SyntaxKind::LParen | SyntaxKind::RParen => {}
                        SyntaxKind::Pattern => {
                            let Some(child) = element.into_node() else { return Err(unsupported()); };
                            push(&mut children, child)?;
                        }
                        _ => return Err(unsupported()),
                    }
                }
                if children.is_empty() { return Err(unsupported()); }
                if !has_comma {
                    if children.len() != 1 { return Err(unsupported()); }
                    return self.case_pattern(&children[0], arm_source, scope, named_group, next_binder, binders);
                }
                let mut elements = Vec::new();
                elements.try_reserve_exact(children.len()).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
                let mut binder_names = std::collections::HashSet::<Box<str>>::new();
                binder_names.try_reserve(children.len()).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
                for child in &children {
                    let element = self.case_pattern_element(&only_child(child)?, arm_source, next_binder, binders)?;
                    if let LocalSourceCasePatternElement::Binder(binder) = &element
                        && !binder_names.insert(binder.spelling.clone())
                    {
                        return Err(unsupported());
                    }
                    elements.push(element);
                }
                Ok(LocalSourceCasePattern::Tuple {
                    source: self.key(pattern)?, range: range_of(pattern), elements,
                })
            }
            _ => Err(unsupported()),
        }
    }

    fn case(
        &mut self,
        node: SyntaxNode,
        index: LocalSourceIndex,
        scope: LocalSourceScope,
        named_group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>,
    ) -> Result<(), FormationError> {
        let parts: Vec<_> = node.children().collect();
        let [scrutinee, block] = parts.as_slice() else { return Err(unsupported()); };
        if scrutinee.kind() != SyntaxKind::CaseScrutinee || block.kind() != SyntaxKind::CaseBlock {
            return Err(unsupported());
        }
        let scrutinee_node = only_child(scrutinee)?;
        let scrutinee_index = self.slot()?;
        let mut arms = Vec::new();
        let mut children = Vec::new();
        for arm in block.children() {
            if arm.kind() == SyntaxKind::CaseArmSeparator { continue; }
            if arm.kind() != SyntaxKind::CaseArm { return Err(unsupported()); }
            let arm_parts: Vec<_> = arm.children().collect();
            let (body, patterns) = arm_parts.split_last().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
            if !matches!(body.kind(), SyntaxKind::OperatorChain | SyntaxKind::IndentedStatementBlock)
                || patterns.len() != 1
                || patterns[0].kind() != SyntaxKind::Pattern
            {
                return Err(unsupported());
            }
            let arm_source = self.key(&arm)?;
            let mut binders = Vec::new();
            let pattern = &patterns[0];
            let mut next_binder = 0;
            let pattern = self.case_pattern(
                pattern, &arm_source, &scope, named_group.clone(), &mut next_binder, &mut binders,
            )?;
            let body_index = self.slot()?;
            push(&mut arms, LocalSourceCaseArm {
                source: arm_source.clone(),
                range: range_of(&arm),
                pattern,
                body: body_index.clone(),
            })?;
            push(&mut children, (body.clone(), body_index, binders))?;
        }
        if arms.is_empty() { return Err(unsupported()); }
        self.set(index.clone(), &node, scope.clone(), named_group.clone(), LocalSourceForm::Case {
            scrutinee: scrutinee_index.clone(),
            arms,
        })?;
        let case_index = index.clone();
        let depth = self.lexical.len();
        for (body, body_index, binders) in children.into_iter().rev() {
            push(&mut self.work, Work::CaseArm(
                body,
                body_index,
                LocalSourceScope::Expression(case_index.clone()),
                binders,
                named_group.clone(),
            ))?;
        }
        push(&mut self.work, Work::Restore(depth))?;
        push(&mut self.work, Work::Expression(scrutinee_node, scrutinee_index, scope, named_group.clone()))?;
        Ok(())
    }
    fn conditional(
        &mut self,
        node: SyntaxNode,
        index: LocalSourceIndex,
        scope: LocalSourceScope,
        named_group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>,
    ) -> Result<(), FormationError> {
        let conditional_scope = LocalSourceScope::Expression(index.clone());
        let mut arms = Vec::new();
        let mut children = Vec::new();
        let mut else_branch = None;
        for arm in node.children() {
            match arm.kind() {
                SyntaxKind::IfArm if else_branch.is_none() => {
                    let parts: Vec<_> = arm.children().collect();
                    let [condition, body] = parts.as_slice() else { return Err(unsupported()); };
                    if condition.kind() != SyntaxKind::Condition
                        || !matches!(body.kind(), SyntaxKind::OperatorChain | SyntaxKind::IndentedStatementBlock | SyntaxKind::BracedStatementBlockExpression)
                    {
                        return Err(unsupported());
                    }
                    let condition_node = only_child(condition)?;
                    if condition_node.kind() != SyntaxKind::OperatorChain { return Err(unsupported()); }
                    let condition_index = self.slot()?;
                    let body_index = self.slot()?;
                    push(&mut arms, (condition_index.clone(), body_index.clone()))?;
                    push(&mut children, (condition_node, condition_index))?;
                    push(&mut children, (body.clone(), body_index))?;
                }
                SyntaxKind::ElseArm if !arms.is_empty() && else_branch.is_none() => {
                    let body = only_child(&arm)?;
                    if !matches!(body.kind(), SyntaxKind::OperatorChain | SyntaxKind::IndentedStatementBlock | SyntaxKind::BracedStatementBlockExpression) {
                        return Err(unsupported());
                    }
                    let body_index = self.slot()?;
                    else_branch = Some(body_index.clone());
                    push(&mut children, (body, body_index))?;
                }
                _ => return Err(unsupported()),
            }
        }
        if arms.is_empty() { return Err(unsupported()); }
        self.set(index, &node, scope, named_group.clone(), LocalSourceForm::If { arms, else_branch })?;
        let depth = self.lexical.len();
        // Every condition and body starts in the enclosing lexical environment.
        for (child, child_index) in children.into_iter().rev() {
            push(&mut self.work, Work::Restore(depth))?;
            if child.kind() == SyntaxKind::BracedStatementBlockExpression
                && record_fields(&child)?.is_none()
                && child.children().filter(|node| node.kind() == SyntaxKind::Statement)
                    .all(|statement| statement.children().next().is_some_and(|node| node.kind() == SyntaxKind::OperatorChain)) {
                self.sequence(child, child_index, conditional_scope.clone(), named_group.clone())?;
            } else {
                push(&mut self.work, Work::Expression(child, child_index, conditional_scope.clone(), named_group.clone()))?;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn sequence(
        &mut self,
        node: SyntaxNode,
        index: LocalSourceIndex,
        scope: LocalSourceScope,
        named_group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>,
    ) -> Result<(), FormationError> {
        let mut statements = Vec::new();
        let mut expect_statement = true;
        for child in node.children() {
            if expect_statement && child.kind() == SyntaxKind::Statement {
                let expression = only_child(&child)?;
                if expression.kind() != SyntaxKind::OperatorChain { return Err(unsupported()); }
                push(&mut statements, expression)?;
            } else if !expect_statement && child.kind() == SyntaxKind::BlockStatementSeparator {
                if child.children().next().is_some() { return Err(unsupported()); }
            } else {
                return Err(unsupported());
            }
            expect_statement = !expect_statement;
        }
        if statements.is_empty() { return Err(unsupported()); }
        let mut items = Vec::new();
        items.try_reserve_exact(statements.len()).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
        for _ in &statements { push(&mut items, self.slot()?)?; }
        let mut retained_items = Vec::new();
        retained_items.try_reserve_exact(items.len()).map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
        retained_items.extend(items.iter().cloned());
        self.set(index.clone(), &node, scope, named_group.clone(), LocalSourceForm::Sequence { items: retained_items })?;
        let sequence_scope = LocalSourceScope::Expression(index);
        let depth = self.lexical.len();
        for (child, child_index) in statements.into_iter().zip(items).rev() {
            push(&mut self.work, Work::Restore(depth))?;
            push(&mut self.work, Work::Expression(child, child_index, sequence_scope.clone(), named_group.clone()))?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn block(
        &mut self,
        node: SyntaxNode,
        index: LocalSourceIndex,
        scope: LocalSourceScope,
        named_group: Option<LocalSourceNamedGroupId>,
    ) -> Result<(), FormationError> {
        let mut statements = Vec::new();
        let mut expect_statement = true;
        let mut seen_statement = false;
        for child in node.children() {
            if child.kind() == SyntaxKind::WhereClause {
                if seen_statement {
                    return Err(unsupported());
                }
                let source = self.key(&child)?;
                if !self
                    .where_clauses
                    .iter()
                    .any(|clause| clause.source == source)
                {
                    return Err(unsupported());
                }
                // A where clause is a preamble child, not one side of the
                // statement/separator alternation emitted for the body.
                continue;
            } else if expect_statement && child.kind() == SyntaxKind::Statement {
                seen_statement = true;
                push(&mut statements, only_child(&child)?)?;
            } else if !expect_statement && child.kind() == SyntaxKind::BlockStatementSeparator {
                if child.children().next().is_some() {
                    return Err(unsupported());
                }
            } else {
                return Err(unsupported());
            }
            expect_statement = !expect_statement;
        }
        let final_node = statements.pop().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
        if final_node.kind() != SyntaxKind::OperatorChain {
            return Err(unsupported());
        }
        let block_scope = LocalSourceScope::Expression(index.clone());
        let final_expression = self.slot()?;
        let mut bindings = Vec::new();
        let mut prefix_work = Vec::new();
        let mut ordered = Vec::new();
        let mut has_expression = false;
        for statement in statements {
            if statement.kind() == SyntaxKind::OperatorChain {
                let expression = self.slot()?;
                push(&mut ordered, LocalSourceStatement::Expression(expression.clone()))?;
                push(&mut prefix_work, Work::Expression(statement, expression, block_scope.clone(), named_group.clone()))?;
                has_expression = true;
                continue;
            }
            if statement.kind() != SyntaxKind::BindingStatement {
                return Err(unsupported());
            }
            let annotation = source_annotation::form(&statement, &self.owner, self.counters)?;
            let (visibility, name, parameters) =
                plain_binding_header(&statement, self.counters).ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
            if visibility != HirVisibility::Private {
                return Err(unsupported());
            }
            body(&statement, HirVisibility::Private)?;
            let ordinal = u32::try_from(self.bindings.len())
                .map_err(|_| HirAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
            let id = HirLocalId {
                owner: self.owner.clone(),
                ordinal,
            };
            let header = statement
                .children()
                .find(|child| child.kind() == SyntaxKind::BindingHeader)
                .ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
            let pattern = only_child(&header)?;
            let binder = pattern.children().next().ok_or_else(unsupported)?;
            let binder_source = self.key(&binder)?;
            self.counters
                .source_identity
                .as_mut()
                .ok_or_else(invalid)?
                .record_local(id.clone(), binder_source.clone());
            let mut retained_parameters = Vec::new();
            let mut parameter_scope = LocalSourceScope::LocalInitializer(id.clone());
            for parameter in parameters {
                let parameter_id = self.parameter_id()?;
                let record_pattern = parameter.record_pattern.as_ref()
                    .map(|node| self.record_parameter_pattern(node, Some(&id))).transpose()?;
                let source = parameter.source.ok_or_else(invalid)?;
                let source_identity = self.counters.source_identity.as_mut().ok_or_else(invalid)?;
                source_identity.record_parameter(parameter_id.clone(), source.clone());
                source_identity
                    .local_parameter_owners
                    .insert(parameter_id.clone(), id.clone());
                push(
                    &mut retained_parameters,
                    LocalSourceParameter {
                        id: parameter_id.clone(),
                        source,
                        range: parameter.name.range,
                        spelling: parameter.name.spelling.into_boxed_str(),
                        unit_pattern: parameter.unit_pattern,
                        record_pattern,
                        annotation: parameter
                            .annotation
                            .as_ref()
                            .map(|node| {
                                source_annotation::parse_annotation(
                                    node,
                                    &self.owner,
                                    self.counters,
                                )
                            })
                            .transpose()?,
                        scope: parameter_scope,
                    },
                )?;
                parameter_scope = LocalSourceScope::Parameter(parameter_id);
            }
            let initializer = self.slot()?;
            push(
                &mut self.bindings,
                LocalSourceBinding {
                    annotation,
                    id,
                    binder_source,
                    range: name.range,
                    spelling: name.spelling.into_boxed_str(),
                    parameters: retained_parameters,
                    initializer,
                    scope: block_scope.clone(),
                },
            )?;
            push(&mut bindings, ordinal)?;
            push(&mut ordered, LocalSourceStatement::Binding(ordinal))?;
            push(&mut prefix_work, Work::Initializer(ordinal, statement, named_group.clone()))?;
        }
        let form = if has_expression {
            LocalSourceForm::StatementBlock { statements: ordered, final_expression: final_expression.clone() }
        } else {
            LocalSourceForm::Block { bindings, final_expression: final_expression.clone() }
        };
        self.set(
            index,
            &node,
            scope,
            named_group.clone(),
            form,
        )?;
        push(&mut self.work, Work::Restore(self.lexical.len()))?;
        push(
            &mut self.work,
            Work::Expression(final_node, final_expression, block_scope, named_group.clone()),
        )?;
        for work in prefix_work.into_iter().rev() {
            push(&mut self.work, work)?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

    pub fn collect(hir: Arc<HirModule>) -> Result<Self, CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        Self::collect_mode(hir, false)
    }

    fn collect_mode(
        hir: Arc<HirModule>,
        candidate_values: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        Self::collect_candidate_mode(hir, candidate_values, false)
    }

    fn collect_candidate_mode(
        hir: Arc<HirModule>,
        candidate_values: bool,
        candidate_graph_effects: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        Self::collect_candidate_mode_scoped(hir, candidate_values, candidate_graph_effects, false)
    }

    fn collect_candidate_mode_scoped(
        hir: Arc<HirModule>, candidate_values: bool, candidate_graph_effects: bool, scoped_experiment: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        Self::collect_candidate_mode_scoped_v1(hir, candidate_values, candidate_graph_effects, scoped_experiment, false)

    fn collect_candidate_mode_scoped_v1(
        hir: Arc<HirModule>, candidate_values: bool, candidate_graph_effects: bool, scoped_experiment: bool, operation_v1: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        Self::collect_candidate_world_scoped_v1(
            vec![hir], candidate_values, candidate_graph_effects, scoped_experiment, operation_v1,
            &HashMap::new(),
            &[],

    fn collect_candidate_world_scoped_v1(
        hirs: Vec<Arc<HirModule>>, candidate_values: bool, candidate_graph_effects: bool,
        scoped_experiment: bool, operation_v1: bool,
        imported_definitions: &HashMap<yu_hir::SourceExportId, DefId>,
        operator_provider_roots: &[candidate_source::OperatorProviderRoot],
    ) -> Result<Self, CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        let hir = hirs.first().cloned().ok_or(CollectionAvailabilityError::NonTotalDefinitionMap)?;
        let hir_count = hirs.len();
        let source_hirs = hirs.clone();
        #[cfg(not(feature = "shadow-apply-candidate"))]
        let _ = (candidate_graph_effects, scoped_experiment);
        let hir_definition_root_allocation_bytes = hirs.iter().map(|hir| hir.definition_root_allocation_bytes()).sum();
        let definition_root_def_id_clone_bytes = hirs.iter().map(|hir| hir.definition_root_def_id_clone_bytes()).sum();
        let mut batch = Self {
            hir: hir.clone(),
            hirs,
            collection_artifact: Arc::new(CollectionArtifactToken),
            projection_order: Vec::new(),
            occurrence_hir_positions: HashMap::new(),
            root_order: Vec::new(),
            root_hir_positions: Vec::new(),
            definitions: Vec::new(),
            definition_positions: HashMap::new(),
            definition_uses: Vec::new(),
            definition_use_positions: HashMap::new(),
            scc_plan: None,
            components: Vec::new(),
            component_terms: Vec::new(),
            component_term_positions: HashMap::new(),
            leaf_terms: HashMap::new(),
            term_builder: Some(TermBuilder::new()?),
            term_arena: None,

===== R274-E025 | crates/yu-solver/src/candidate_source.rs:1043-2383 | source sha256 bb8348a4cf6f83df1099f06bb0e853a178d4629f85778730bb2f394d74745a49 | excerpt sha256 59bedf8a9ebe272c85aa491d31bcc54f1b3b11728b120a88cd49f43f186100d0 | candidate-source-collection-and-schedule =====
    pub(super) fn emit_candidate_source<'a>(
        &mut self,
        source: &'a LocalSource,
        parent: &SourceReceiver,
        hir: &Arc<HirModule>,
        hir_index: usize,
        imported_definitions: &'a HashMap<yu_hir::SourceExportId, DefId>,
        uses: &mut Vec<PendingDefinitionUse<'a>>,
    ) -> Result<(), CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        let owner = source.source_root();
        let original = hir.local_source_for(owner).map_err(|_| invalid())?.ok_or_else(invalid)?;
        if !std::ptr::eq(source, original) { return Err(invalid()); }
        match (parent, owner) {
            (SourceReceiver::Definition(id), SourceRootId::Definition(root))
                if self.definitions.get(id.ordinal() as usize).is_some_and(|record| &record.definition == id && &record.root == root) => {},
            (SourceReceiver::Expression(root), SourceRootId::Expression(actual)) if root == actual => {},
            (SourceReceiver::Member(root), SourceRootId::Member(actual)) if root == actual => {},
            _ => return Err(invalid()),
        }
        self.candidate_source.source_owners.try_reserve(source.expressions().len()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        self.occurrence_hir_positions.try_reserve(source.expressions().len()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        for expr in source.expressions() {
            if !hir.owns_occurrence(&expr.occurrence) { return Err(invalid()); }
            if self.occurrence_hir_positions.insert(expr.occurrence.clone(), hir_index).is_some_and(|previous| previous != hir_index) { return Err(invalid()); }
            if self.candidate_source.source_owners.insert(expr.occurrence.clone(), owner.clone()).is_some_and(|previous| &previous != owner) {
                return Err(invalid());
            }
        }
        // Reuse the graph's existing fragment index. The actual source walk
        // below supplies immutable birth/planning levels before SCC sealing.
        let reference_fragments = self.candidate_source.references.retain_source(source)?;
        let mut positions = Vec::new();
        positions.try_reserve_exact(source.expressions().len()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        let mut parameters = HashMap::new();
        let mut local_slots = HashMap::new();
        let mut lambdas = HashMap::new();
        let mut formal_registrations = HashMap::new();
        let mut parameter_scopes = HashMap::new();
        let mut catch_arm_scopes = HashMap::new();
        for parameter in source.root_parameters() {
            let position = self.parameter_recipes.len();
            push(&mut self.parameter_recipes, parameter.id.clone())?;
            parameters.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
            parameters.insert(parameter.id.clone(), position);
            self.candidate_source.parameter_levels.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
            self.candidate_source.parameter_levels.insert(position, 1);
            self.candidate_source.references.record_birth(
                &candidate_value_graph::Value::Parameter(parameter.id.clone()), 1)?;
        }
        for binding in source.bindings() {
            parameter_scopes.try_reserve(binding.parameters.len()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
            for parameter in &binding.parameters {
                parameter_scopes.insert(parameter.id.clone(), candidate_effect::AnnotationScope::Local(binding.id.clone()));
            }
        }
        for (index, expr) in source.expressions().iter().enumerate() {
            positions.push(self.candidate_component(&expr.occurrence)?);
            if let LocalSourceForm::Catch { arms, .. } = &expr.form {
                catch_arm_scopes.try_reserve(arms.len()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                for arm in arms { catch_arm_scopes.insert(arm.source.clone(), candidate_effect::AnnotationScope::Expression(arm.body.clone())); }
            }
            if let LocalSourceForm::Lambda { parameter, .. } = &expr.form {
                let position = self.parameter_recipes.len();
                push(&mut self.parameter_recipes, parameter.id.clone())?;
                parameters.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                parameters.insert(parameter.id.clone(), position);
                let registration = self.candidate_calls.formals.len();
                push(&mut self.candidate_calls.formals, candidate_call::FormalRegistrationInput {
                    owner: owner.clone(), lambda: index,
                    parameter: parameter.id.clone(), recipe_position: position, native_root: None,
                    role: if parameter.annotation.as_ref().is_some_and(|a| a.ty.effects.is_some()) { candidate_call::FormalRole::Computation } else { candidate_call::FormalRole::Value },
                    entry_effect: None, receiver_body: None,
                    scope: parameter_scopes.get(&parameter.id).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| candidate_effect::AnnotationScope::for_source(owner.clone())),
                })?;
                formal_registrations.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                formal_registrations.insert(parameter.id.clone(), registration);
                for field in parameter.record_pattern.iter().flatten() {
                    let position = self.parameter_recipes.len();
                    push(&mut self.parameter_recipes, field.binder.clone())?;
                    parameters.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    if parameters.insert(field.binder.clone(), position).is_some() { return Err(invalid()); }
                }
            }
        }
        for binding in source.bindings() {
            let slot = self.candidate_source.locals.len();
            push(&mut self.candidate_source.locals, binding.id.clone())?;
            local_slots.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
            local_slots.insert(binding.id.clone(), slot);
        }
        let mut endpoints = Vec::new();
        endpoints.try_reserve_exact(positions.len()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        for (index, expr) in source.expressions().iter().enumerate() {
            let endpoint = match &expr.form {
                LocalSourceForm::Name { resolution: LocalSourceResolution::Parameter(parameter), .. } =>
                    CandidateEndpoint::Parameter(*parameters.get(parameter).ok_or_else(invalid)?),
                _ => CandidateEndpoint::Component(positions[index].value),
            };
            endpoints.push(endpoint);
        }
        let mut ascription_children = HashMap::new();
        // Resolve aliases before recursive initializer links capture endpoints.
        // Group facades stay intact; only an ascription peels parentheses.
        for (index, expr) in source.expressions().iter().enumerate() {
            if let LocalSourceForm::Ascription { inner, .. } = &expr.form {
                let mut child = inner.ordinal() as usize;
                loop {
                    match &source.expressions()[child].form {
                        LocalSourceForm::Ascription { inner, .. } | LocalSourceForm::Group { inner } => child = inner.ordinal() as usize,
                        _ => break,
                    }
                }
                ascription_children.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                ascription_children.insert(index, child);
                positions[index] = positions[child];
                *self.occurrence_component_positions.get_mut(&expr.occurrence).ok_or_else(invalid)? = positions[child];
                endpoints[index] = endpoints[child];
            }
        }
        let mut formal_names = Vec::new();
        formal_names.try_reserve_exact(positions.len()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        formal_names.resize(positions.len(), None);
        for (index, expr) in source.expressions().iter().enumerate() {
            if let LocalSourceForm::Name { resolution: LocalSourceResolution::Parameter(parameter), .. } = &expr.form {
                // Member receivers are source parameters too, but are not lambda
                // formals and therefore have no candidate-call registration.
                if let Some(&registration) = formal_registrations.get(parameter) {
                    let name = self.candidate_calls.names.len();
                    push(&mut self.candidate_calls.names, candidate_call::FormalNameInput {
                        owner: owner.clone(), expression: index,
                        registration,
                    })?;
                    formal_names[index] = Some(name);
                }
            }
        }
        let mut catch_positions = HashMap::new();
        let mut case_positions = HashMap::new();
        for (index, expr) in source.expressions().iter().enumerate() {
            if let LocalSourceForm::Name { resolution: LocalSourceResolution::CatchBinder(id), .. } = &expr.form {
                catch_positions.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                catch_positions.entry(id.clone()).or_insert(positions[index].value);
            }
            if let LocalSourceForm::Name { resolution: LocalSourceResolution::CaseBinder(id), .. } = &expr.form {
                case_positions.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                case_positions.entry(id.clone()).or_insert(positions[index].value);
            }
        }
        let mut catch_binders = HashMap::new();
        let mut case_binders = HashMap::new();
        let mut case_guards = Vec::new();
        let mut actions = Vec::new();
        let mut field_bound_v1 = Vec::new();
        let mut work = Vec::new();
        // Only currently forming function initializers use an open monomorphic
        // root. Published locals continue through the ordinary scheme route.
        let mut active_initializers = HashMap::new();
        active_initializers.try_reserve(source.bindings().len()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        let definition_region = self.candidate_source.region(None)?;
        if self.candidate_source.scoped_experiment {
            self.candidate_source.definition_regions.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
            if let SourceRootId::Definition(definition) = owner {
                self.candidate_source.definition_regions.insert(definition.clone(), definition_region);
            }
        }
        push(&mut work, Work::Visit(source.body().ordinal() as usize, 1, definition_region))?;
        while let Some(next) = work.pop() {
            match next {
                Work::Visit(index, level, region) => {
                    self.candidate_source.computation_scope(&source.expressions()[index].occurrence, region)?;
                    let expr = source.expressions().get(index).ok_or_else(invalid)?;
                    self.candidate_source.references.record_expression_level(
                        &expr.occurrence, reference_fragments[index], level)?;
                    if let LocalSourceForm::Name { resolution: LocalSourceResolution::CatchBinder(binder), .. } = &expr.form {
                        endpoints[index] = *catch_binders.get(binder).ok_or_else(invalid)?;
                    }
                    if let LocalSourceForm::Name { resolution: LocalSourceResolution::CaseBinder(binder), .. } = &expr.form {
                        endpoints[index] = *case_binders.get(binder).ok_or_else(invalid)?;
                    }
                    let pos = positions[index];
                    self.candidate_source.component_levels.try_reserve(2).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    // A Catch binder's representative is introduced at the arm level;
                    // its first Name can occur inside a deeper initializer.
                    self.candidate_source.component_levels.entry(pos.value).or_insert(level);
                    self.candidate_source.component_levels.insert(pos.effect, level);
                    push(&mut work, Work::Finish(index, level))?;
                    match &expr.form {
                        LocalSourceForm::Catch { scrutinee, .. } => {
                            push(&mut work, Work::CatchArms(index, level, region))?;
                            push(&mut work, Work::Visit(scrutinee.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?;
                        }
                        LocalSourceForm::Case { scrutinee, .. } => {
                            // LIFO must visit the scrutinee before CaseArms copies its
                            // endpoint into arm binders. CaseArms owns the only visits
                            // of the arm patterns and bodies.
                            push(&mut work, Work::CaseArms(index, level, region))?;
                            push(&mut work, Work::Visit(scrutinee.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?;
                        }
                        LocalSourceForm::If { arms, else_branch } => {
                            if let Some(else_branch) = else_branch {
                                push(&mut work, Work::Visit(else_branch.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?;
                            }
                            for (condition, body) in arms.iter().rev() {
                                push(&mut work, Work::Visit(body.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?;
                                push(&mut work, Work::Visit(condition.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?;
                            }
                        }
                        LocalSourceForm::Sequence { items } => {
                            for item in items.iter().rev() {
                                push(&mut work, Work::Visit(item.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?;
                            }
                        }
                        LocalSourceForm::Variant { payloads, .. } => {
                            for payload in payloads.iter().rev() { push(&mut work, Work::Visit(payload.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?; }
                        }
                        LocalSourceForm::InterpolatedString { lowered: None, .. } => return Err(unavailable()),
                        LocalSourceForm::Record { fields } => {
                            for field in fields.iter().rev() { push(&mut work, Work::Visit(field.value.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?; }
                        }
                        LocalSourceForm::Tuple { elements } => {
                            for element in elements.iter().rev() {
                                push(&mut work, Work::Visit(element.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?;
                            }
                        }
                        LocalSourceForm::FieldTail { receiver, .. } | LocalSourceForm::GeneratedFormatSelection { receiver, .. } => {
                            push(&mut work, Work::Visit(receiver.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?;
                        }
                        LocalSourceForm::Apply { callee, argument, .. } => {
                            push(&mut work, Work::Visit(argument.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?;
                            push(&mut work, Work::Visit(callee.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?;
                        }
                        LocalSourceForm::OperatorApplication { left, right, .. } => {
                            if let Some(right) = right { push(&mut work, Work::Visit(right.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?; }
                            push(&mut work, Work::Visit(left.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?;
                        }
                        LocalSourceForm::Group { inner } | LocalSourceForm::Ascription { inner, .. }
                        | LocalSourceForm::InterpolatedString { lowered: Some(inner), .. } => push(&mut work, Work::Visit(inner.ordinal() as usize, level, region))?,
                        LocalSourceForm::Lambda { parameter, body } => {
                            push(&mut actions, Action::BeginLambda)?;
                            let position = *parameters.get(&parameter.id).ok_or_else(invalid)?;
                            self.candidate_source.parameter_levels.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                            self.candidate_source.parameter_levels.insert(position, level);
