                        role: yu_hir::shadow::SourceRoleId {
                            module: Arc::new(root.module().clone()),
                            declaration: root.declaration_source_key().clone(),
                        },
                        declaration: root.member_source_key().clone(),
                    }),
                    yu_hir::SourceMemberKind::Implementation => None,
                },
                implementation_member: match root.kind() {
                    yu_hir::SourceMemberKind::Role => None,
                    yu_hir::SourceMemberKind::Implementation => Some(
                        yu_hir::shadow::SourceImplementationMemberId {
                            implementation: yu_hir::shadow::SourceImplementationId {
                                module: Arc::new(root.module().clone()),
                                declaration: root.declaration_source_key().clone(),
                            },
                            declaration: root.member_source_key().clone(),
                        },
                    ),
                },
                header: header.clone(),
                receiver, components: positions[body], value: endpoints[body], actions,
            });
        }
        self.candidate_source.active = true;
        Ok(())
    }
    fn append_source_facts(&self, start: usize, actions: &mut Vec<Action>) -> Result<(), CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        for index in start..self.occurrences.len() { push(actions, Action::Fact(index))?; }
        Ok(())
    }
    pub(super) fn candidate_legacy_actions(&self, start: usize, end: usize, candidate_start: usize, lambda_start: usize) -> Result<Vec<Action>, CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        let mut actions = Vec::new();
        let mut routed = HashSet::new();
        let mut candidate = candidate_start;
        let mut lambda = lambda_start;
        for index in start..=end {
            while self.candidate_recipes.get(candidate).is_some_and(|r| r.after_collected_fact == index) {
                push(&mut actions, Action::Candidate(candidate))?;
                candidate += 1;
            }
            while self.lambda_recipes.get(lambda).is_some_and(|r| r.after_collected_fact == index) {
                push(&mut actions, Action::Lambda(lambda))?;
                lambda += 1;
            }
            if index < end {
                let occurrence = self.occurrences[index].id.occurrence();
                routed.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                if routed.insert(occurrence.clone()) { push(&mut actions, Action::Module(occurrence.clone()))?; }
                push(&mut actions, Action::Fact(index))?;
            }
        }
        Ok(actions)
    }

}
impl InferenceSession {
    pub(super) fn initialize_candidate_source_levels(&mut self) -> Result<(), SolveAvailabilityError> {
        for (&component, &level) in &self.batch.candidate_source.component_levels {
            let endpoint = self.live_components[component];
            match endpoint.kind {
                ComponentKind::Value => self.value_levels[endpoint.ordinal as usize] = level,
                ComponentKind::Effect => self.effect_levels[endpoint.ordinal as usize] = level,
            }
        }
        for (&parameter, &level) in &self.batch.candidate_source.parameter_levels {
            self.value_levels[self.parameter_live_base as usize + parameter] = level;
        }
        for index in 0..self.batch.candidate_calls.formals.len() {
            let registration = &self.batch.candidate_calls.formals[index];
            if self
                .batch
                .parameter_recipes
                .get(registration.recipe_position)
                != Some(&registration.parameter)
                || registration.native_root.is_some()
            {
                return Err(SolveAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted);
            }
            let parameter = registration.recipe_position;
            let role = registration.role;
            let root = self.candidate_endpoint(
                CandidateEndpoint::Parameter(parameter),
                Polarity::Positive,
            )?;
            let entry = if role == candidate_call::FormalRole::Computation {
                let level = *self.batch.candidate_source.parameter_levels.get(&parameter).ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
                Some(self.fresh_effect_at_level(level)?)
            } else { None };
            self.batch.candidate_calls.formals[index].native_root = Some(root);
            self.batch.candidate_calls.formals[index].entry_effect = entry;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    pub(super) fn execute_candidate_expression(&mut self, index: usize) -> Result<(), SolveAvailabilityError> {
        let schedule = self.batch.candidate_source.expressions.get(index).ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::ReceiptMismatch)?;
        if self.batch.candidate_source.source_owners.get(schedule.root.occurrence()) != Some(&SourceRootId::Expression(schedule.root.clone()))
            || self.batch.candidate_source.component_levels.get(&schedule.components.value) != Some(&1)
            || self.batch.candidate_source.component_levels.get(&schedule.components.effect) != Some(&1)
        { return Err(SolveAvailabilityError::ReceiptMismatch); }
        let value = schedule.value;
        self.candidate_endpoint(value, Polarity::Positive)?;
        self.execute_candidate_source_schedule(Schedule::Expression(index))
    }
    pub(super) fn execute_candidate_member(&mut self, index: usize) -> Result<(), SolveAvailabilityError> {
        let schedule = self.batch.candidate_source.members.get(index).ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::ReceiptMismatch)?;
        let owner = SourceRootId::Member(schedule.root.clone());
        let CandidateEndpoint::Parameter(receiver) = schedule.receiver else { return Err(SolveAvailabilityError::ReceiptMismatch); };
        if self.batch.candidate_source.source_owners.get(&schedule.occurrence) != Some(&owner)
            || self.batch.parameter_recipes.get(receiver).is_none_or(|parameter| parameter.source_root() != &owner)
            || self.batch.candidate_source.parameter_levels.get(&receiver).is_none()
            || !self.batch.candidate_source.component_levels.contains_key(&schedule.components.value)
            || !self.batch.candidate_source.component_levels.contains_key(&schedule.components.effect)
        { return Err(SolveAvailabilityError::ReceiptMismatch); }
        self.execute_candidate_source_schedule(Schedule::Member(index))
    }
    pub(super) fn execute_candidate_loose(&mut self) -> Result<(), SolveAvailabilityError> {
        self.execute_candidate_source_schedule(Schedule::Loose)
    }
    pub(super) fn execute_candidate_source_root(&mut self, root: &DefinitionRootId) -> Result<(), SolveAvailabilityError> {
        let position = *self.batch.root_definition_positions.get(root).ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
        self.execute_candidate_source_schedule(Schedule::Root(position))
    }
    fn execute_candidate_source_schedule(&mut self, schedule: Schedule) -> Result<(), SolveAvailabilityError> {
        self.begin_candidate_source(schedule)?;
        // Completed-result callers never attach invalidatable synthetic support.
        debug_assert!(self.candidate_graph.as_ref().unwrap().source.unwrap().registration.is_none());
        let result = self.drive_candidate_source(Support::READY);
        if result.is_err() { self.candidate_graph.as_mut().unwrap().source = None; }
        match result? {
            CandidateRun::Complete(()) => Ok(()),
            CandidateRun::Suspended(_) => unreachable!("synchronous source caller supplies supported execution"),
        }
    }
    pub(super) fn begin_candidate_source(&mut self, schedule: Schedule) -> Result<(), SolveAvailabilityError> {
        let state = self.candidate_graph.as_mut().ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
        if state.source.is_some() || !state.local_capture_stack.is_empty() || state.source_lambda_depth != 0 {
            return Err(SolveAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted);
        }
        state.source = Some(Frame { schedule, next: 0, stage: Stage::Execute, wait: None, registration: None });
        if let Err(error) = self.sample_f4_resources(ResourceBoundary::SourceDrafts) {
            self.candidate_graph.as_mut().unwrap().source = None;
            return Err(error);
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    // This private driver retains the session in `self`. Until inner operations
    // have retained outcomes, support may only change at completed action or
    // construction boundaries. Production certificate creation remains disabled.

    pub(super) fn drive_candidate_source(&mut self, support: Support) -> Result<CandidateRun<()>, SolveAvailabilityError> {
        loop {
            match self.step_candidate_source(support)? {
                CandidateRun::Complete(true) => return Ok(CandidateRun::Complete(())),
                CandidateRun::Complete(false) => {},
                CandidateRun::Suspended(wait) => return Ok(CandidateRun::Suspended(wait)),
            }
        }
    }
    pub(super) fn step_candidate_source(&mut self, support: Support) -> Result<CandidateRun<bool>, SolveAvailabilityError> {
        let frame = self.candidate_graph.as_ref().and_then(|state| state.source).ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
        if let Some(registration) = frame.registration {
            if let Some(wait) = self.candidate_graph.as_ref().unwrap().intrusion.effect_algebra.context.source_support_wait(registration)? {
                self.candidate_graph.as_mut().unwrap().source.as_mut().unwrap().wait = Some(wait);
                return Ok(CandidateRun::Suspended(wait));
            }
        }
        if let Some(wait) = support.wait {
            self.candidate_graph.as_mut().unwrap().source.as_mut().unwrap().wait = Some(wait);
            return Ok(CandidateRun::Suspended(wait));
        }
        self.candidate_graph.as_mut().unwrap().source.as_mut().unwrap().wait = None;
        if let Stage::PublishLocal { slot, exposed, boundary } = frame.stage {
            self.install_candidate_local_term(slot, exposed, boundary)?;
            let current = self.candidate_graph.as_mut().unwrap().source.as_mut().unwrap();
            current.stage = Stage::Execute;
            current.registration = None;
            current.next += 1;
            return Ok(CandidateRun::Complete(false));
        }
        let actions = match frame.schedule {
            Schedule::Loose => &self.batch.candidate_source.loose,
            Schedule::Expression(index) => &self.batch.candidate_source.expressions.get(index).ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::ReceiptMismatch)?.actions,
            Schedule::Member(index) => &self.batch.candidate_source.members.get(index).ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::ReceiptMismatch)?.actions,
            Schedule::Root(position) => {
                let root = &self.batch.definitions.get(position).ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?.root;
                self.batch.candidate_source.schedules.get(root).ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?
            }
        };
        let Some(action) = actions.get(frame.next).cloned() else {
            let state = self.candidate_graph.as_ref().unwrap();
            if !state.local_capture_stack.is_empty() || state.source_lambda_depth != 0 {
                return Err(SolveAvailabilityError::ReceiptMismatch);
            }
            self.candidate_graph.as_mut().unwrap().source = None;
            return Ok(CandidateRun::Complete(true));
        };
        match &action {
            Action::Install { slot, initializer, boundary } => {
                let exposed = self.candidate_endpoint(*initializer, Polarity::Positive)?;
                self.candidate_graph.as_mut().unwrap().source.as_mut().unwrap().stage = Stage::PublishLocal { slot: *slot, exposed, boundary: *boundary };
                return Ok(CandidateRun::Complete(false));
            }
            Action::LocalAnnotation { annotation, slot, endpoint, computation_effect, occurrence, level, boundary } => {
                let exposed = self.construct_candidate_local_annotation(annotation, *slot, *endpoint, occurrence, *level, *computation_effect)?;
                self.candidate_graph.as_mut().unwrap().source.as_mut().unwrap().stage = Stage::PublishLocal { slot: *slot, exposed, boundary: *boundary };
                return Ok(CandidateRun::Complete(false));
            }
            _ => {},
        }
===== END R274-E026; omitted surrounding regions are NOT evidence or assumed no-ops =====
===== R274-E027 | crates/yu-solver/src/candidate_source.rs:2469-2523 | source sha256 bb8348a4cf6f83df1099f06bb0e853a178d4629f85778730bb2f394d74745a49 | excerpt sha256 1dbf4284df784410f25992bf3ea83c13a95d944394eef81578bb09f43da5f030 | candidate-action-dispatch =====
    pub(super) fn execute_candidate_action(&mut self, action: &Action) -> Result<(), SolveAvailabilityError> {
            match action {
                Action::Ascription { annotation, endpoint, computation_effect, occurrence, level, scope } => self.candidate_expression_ascription(annotation, *endpoint, occurrence, *level, *computation_effect, scope)?,
                Action::FormalAnnotation { annotation, parameter, occurrence, scope } => self.candidate_formal_annotation(annotation, *parameter, occurrence, scope)?,
                Action::UnitParameter { parameter, occurrence } => self.candidate_unit_parameter(*parameter, occurrence)?,
                Action::RecordParameter { parameter, fields, occurrence } => self.candidate_record_parameter(*parameter, fields, occurrence)?,
                Action::FormalEntry { registration, annotation, occurrence, scope } => self.candidate_formal_entry_annotation(*registration, annotation, occurrence, scope)?,
                Action::FormalNameEffect { registration, occurrence, effect } => {
                    let entry = self.batch.candidate_calls.formals[*registration].entry_effect.ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
                    let name = self.live_components[*effect].ordinal;
                    self.candidate_formal_effect_equal(occurrence, 1, entry, name)?;
                }
                Action::Fact(index) => self.admit_collected_fact(*index)?,
                Action::Link { occurrence, endpoint, target } => {
                    let lower = self.candidate_endpoint(*endpoint, Polarity::Positive)?;
                    let upper = self.batch.component_term_at(*target);
                    self.admit_candidate_value_link(occurrence, 30, lower, upper)?;
                }
                Action::ValueConstraint { occurrence, slot, endpoint, upper } => {
                    let lower = self.candidate_endpoint(*endpoint, Polarity::Positive)?;
                    self.admit_candidate_value_link(occurrence, *slot, lower, *upper)?;
                }
                Action::Candidate(index) => { let recipe = self.batch.candidate_recipes[*index].clone(); self.admit_candidate_fact(&recipe)?; }
                Action::Operator(index) => self.execute_candidate_operator_use(*index)?,
                Action::Lambda(index) => { let recipe = self.batch.lambda_recipes[*index].clone(); self.admit_lambda_fact(&recipe)?; }
                Action::BeginLambda => {
                    let state = self.candidate_graph.as_mut().ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::ReceiptMismatch)?;
                    state.source_lambda_depth = state.source_lambda_depth.checked_add(1).ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted)?;
                }
                Action::EndLambda => {
                    let state = self.candidate_graph.as_mut().ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::ReceiptMismatch)?;
                    state.source_lambda_depth = state.source_lambda_depth.checked_sub(1).ok_or(SolveAvailabilityError::ReceiptMismatch)?;
                }
                Action::Module(occurrence) => {
                    let id = DefinitionUseId::new(self.batch.collection_artifact.clone(), occurrence.clone());
                    if self.batch.definition_use_positions.contains_key(&id) {
                        if self.candidate_graph.as_ref().is_some_and(|state| state.intrusion.active_uses.contains(&id)) { self.route_candidate_open_use(&id)?; }
                        else { self.route_incoming(&id)?; }
                    }
                }
                Action::LocalAnnotation { annotation, slot, endpoint, computation_effect, occurrence, level, boundary } => self.candidate_local_annotation(annotation, *slot, *endpoint, occurrence, *level, *boundary, *computation_effect)?,
                Action::Local { slot, occurrence, value, level } => self.route_candidate_local(*slot, occurrence, *value, *level)?,
                Action::BeginLocal { slot } => self.begin_candidate_local_capture(*slot)?,
                Action::Install { slot, initializer, boundary } => self.install_candidate_local(*slot, *initializer, *boundary)?,
                Action::CatchOperation { declaration, owner, occurrence, payload, continuation, original, effect, level, h_arg } => self.candidate_catch_operation(declaration, owner, occurrence, *payload, *continuation, *original, *effect, *level, h_arg.as_ref())?,
                Action::FieldBoundV1 { declaration, h_arg } => self.candidate_field_bound_v1(declaration, h_arg)?,
                Action::Operation { declaration, owner, occurrence, target, level } => self.candidate_operation(declaration, owner, occurrence, *target, *level)?,
                Action::Primitive { operation, owner, position, occurrence, target, level } => self.candidate_pinned_primitive(operation.clone(), owner, position, occurrence, *target, *level)?,
                Action::Annotation { annotation, endpoint, computation_effect, target, occurrence, level } => self.candidate_annotation(annotation, *endpoint, *target, occurrence, *level, *computation_effect)?,
                Action::WhereClause { source, scope } => self.candidate_register_source_role_obligation(source, scope)?,
            }
        Ok(())
    }
}

===== R274-E028 | crates/yu-solver/src/candidate_value_graph.rs:1-415 | source sha256 aaaa88a374083f898188cd390b8cf0244855d8c09c8939b9e68ee2831bc692cf | excerpt sha256 6cbc0c89b0164a94f958333c3f3fb7580f668ef6a341f64a9a551cef1d0cb0ab | candidate-value-graph-and-seal =====
//! Source Value references, independent of solver rows and copied descriptions.
use crate::*;
use yu_hir::{HirLocalId, shadow::{LocalSource, LocalSourceForm, LocalSourceResolution}};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Eq, Hash, PartialEq)]
pub(super) enum Value { Module(DefId), Local(HirLocalId), Parameter(HirParameterId), CatchBinder(yu_hir::shadow::LocalSourceCatchBinderId), Expression(HirOccurrenceId) }
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub(super) struct Association { pub origin: usize, pub receiver: usize }
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
pub(super) struct Graph {
    modules: HashMap<DefinitionRootId, DefId>,
    vertices: HashMap<Value, usize>,
    values_by_ordinal: HashMap<usize, Value>,
    edges: Vec<Vec<usize>>,
    reverse: Vec<Vec<usize>>,
    // Semantic vertices retain immutable source birth. Helpers retain the
    // actual planning level of their expression, not a guessed Value birth.
    planning_levels: Vec<Option<u32>>,
    pub events: Vec<Association>,
    pub checking: HashMap<ConstraintOccurrenceId, usize>,
    checking_by_event: Vec<ConstraintOccurrenceId>,
    components: Vec<usize>,
    edge_bytes: usize,
    identity_bytes: usize,
    copied_module_payload_bytes: usize,
    pub seal_scratch_peak: usize,
    pub construction_scratch_peak: usize,
}
fn unavailable() -> CollectionAvailabilityError { CollectionAvailabilityError::ComponentIdentityExhausted }
impl Graph {
    // Observation only: auxiliary fragment vertices have no Value descriptor.
    pub fn observed_value(&self, ordinal: usize) -> Option<(&Value, usize)> {
        let value = self.values_by_ordinal.get(&ordinal)?;
        Some((value, *self.components.get(ordinal)?))
    }
    pub fn value_component(&self, value: &Value) -> Option<usize> {
        self.vertices.get(value).and_then(|ordinal| self.components.get(*ordinal)).copied()
    }
    pub fn observed_checking(&self, event: usize) -> Option<&ConstraintOccurrenceId> {
        let checking = self.checking_by_event.get(event)?;
        (self.checking.get(checking) == Some(&event)).then_some(checking)
    }
    pub fn register_module(&mut self, root: DefinitionRootId, id: DefId) -> Result<(), CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        self.modules.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        self.identity_bytes = checked_usize_sum([self.identity_bytes, id.hash_eq_payload_bytes()], "Value module identity");
        self.modules.insert(root, id); Ok(())
    }
    fn intern(&mut self, value: Value) -> Result<usize, CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        if let Some(&id) = self.vertices.get(&value) { return Ok(id); }
        self.vertices.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        self.values_by_ordinal.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        self.edges.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        self.reverse.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        self.planning_levels.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        // Every module source body is planned at level 1. Forward Names may
        // intern this identity before its body is visited.
        let birth = matches!(&value, Value::Module(_)).then_some(1);
        let module_payload_bytes = match &value {
            Value::Module(id) => id.hash_eq_payload_bytes(),
            _ => 0,
        };
        if let Value::Module(id) = &value { self.identity_bytes = checked_usize_sum([self.identity_bytes, id.hash_eq_payload_bytes()], "Value identity"); }
        self.copied_module_payload_bytes = checked_usize_sum(
            [self.copied_module_payload_bytes, module_payload_bytes],
            "Value ordinal module payload",
        );
        let id = self.edges.len();
        let reverse_value = value.clone();
        self.vertices.insert(value, id);
        self.values_by_ordinal.insert(id, reverse_value);
        self.edges.push(Vec::new()); self.reverse.push(Vec::new());
        self.planning_levels.push(birth);
        Ok(id)
    }
    fn record_level(&mut self, vertex: usize, level: u32) -> Result<(), CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        let stored = self.planning_levels.get_mut(vertex).ok_or_else(unavailable)?;
        match *stored {
            Some(previous) if previous != level => Err(unavailable()),
            Some(_) => Ok(()),
            None => { *stored = Some(level); Ok(()) }
        }
    }
    pub(super) fn record_birth(&mut self, value: &Value, level: u32) -> Result<(), CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        // Unused declarations need not add an isolated graph vertex. Every
        // actual Name/receiver was interned by retain_source before this walk.
        if let Some(&vertex) = self.vertices.get(value) { self.record_level(vertex, level)?; }
        Ok(())
    }
    pub(super) fn record_expression_level(&mut self, occurrence: &HirOccurrenceId,
        fragment: Option<usize>, level: u32) -> Result<(), CollectionAvailabilityError>
    {
        self.record_birth(&Value::Expression(occurrence.clone()), level)?;
        if let Some(fragment) = fragment { self.record_level(fragment, level)?; }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn edge(&mut self, from: usize, to: usize) -> Result<(), CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        let previous = self.edges[from].capacity() + self.reverse[to].capacity();
        self.edges[from].try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        self.reverse[to].try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        self.edge_bytes = checked_usize_sum([self.edge_bytes, checked_capacity_bytes::<usize>(self.edges[from].capacity() + self.reverse[to].capacity() - previous, "Value edges")], "Value edges");
        self.edges[from].push(to); self.reverse[to].push(from); Ok(())
    }
    fn resolved(resolution: &LocalSourceResolution) -> Option<Value> {
        match resolution { LocalSourceResolution::ModuleDef(id) => Some(Value::Module(id.clone())),
            LocalSourceResolution::Local(id) => Some(Value::Local(id.clone())),
            LocalSourceResolution::CatchBinder(id) => Some(Value::CatchBinder(id.clone())),
            LocalSourceResolution::CaseBinder(id) => Some(Value::CatchBinder(id.clone())),
            LocalSourceResolution::Parameter(id) => Some(Value::Parameter(id.clone())), _ => None }
    }
    fn expression_value(source: &LocalSource, mut index: usize) -> Value {
        loop { let expr = &source.expressions()[index]; match &expr.form {
            LocalSourceForm::Group { inner } | LocalSourceForm::Ascription { inner, .. } => index = inner.ordinal() as usize,
            LocalSourceForm::Name { resolution, .. } => return Self::resolved(resolution).unwrap_or_else(|| Value::Expression(expr.occurrence.clone())),
            _ => return Value::Expression(expr.occurrence.clone()),
        }}
    }
    pub fn retain_source(&mut self, source: &LocalSource) -> Result<Vec<Option<usize>>, CollectionAvailabilityError> {
        let value = match source.source_root() {
            SourceRootId::Definition(definition) => Value::Module(self.modules.get(definition).ok_or(CollectionAvailabilityError::MissingDefinitionEndpoint)?.clone()),
            SourceRootId::Expression(root) => Value::Expression(root.occurrence().clone()),
            SourceRootId::Member(_) => {
                let body = source.expressions().get(source.body().ordinal() as usize)
                    .ok_or(CollectionAvailabilityError::MissingDefinitionEndpoint)?;
                Value::Expression(body.occurrence.clone())
            }
        };
        let root = self.intern(value)?;
        self.record_level(root, 1)?;
        let mut owners = Vec::new(); owners.try_reserve_exact(source.expressions().len()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        owners.resize(source.expressions().len(), root);
        let mut work = Vec::new(); work.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?; work.push((source.body().ordinal() as usize, root));
        // Tree construction assigns nested initializers to their own declaration.
        while let Some((index, owner)) = work.pop() {
            owners[index] = owner;
            let expr = &source.expressions()[index];
            match &expr.form {
                LocalSourceForm::Name { resolution, .. } => if let Some(value) = Self::resolved(resolution) {
                    let target = self.intern(value)?; self.edge(owner, target)?;
                },
                LocalSourceForm::Catch { scrutinee, arms } => {
                    work.try_reserve(arms.len().checked_add(1).ok_or_else(unavailable)?).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    work.push((scrutinee.ordinal() as usize, owner));
                    for arm in arms {
                        use yu_hir::shadow::{LocalSourceCatchPattern as Pattern, LocalSourceCatchPayloadPattern as Payload, LocalSourceCatchValuePattern as PatternValue};
                        let mut retain = |pattern: &PatternValue| -> Result<(), CollectionAvailabilityError> {
                            if let PatternValue::Binder(binder) = pattern { self.intern(Value::CatchBinder(binder.id.clone()))?; }
                            Ok(())
                        };
                        match &arm.pattern {
                            Pattern::Value(pattern) => retain(pattern)?,
                            Pattern::Operation { payloads, continuation, .. } => {
                                for payload in payloads { if let Payload::Value(pattern) = payload { retain(pattern)?; } }
                                retain(continuation)?;
                            }
                        }
                        work.push((arm.body.ordinal() as usize, owner));
                    }
                },
                LocalSourceForm::Case { scrutinee, arms } => {
                    work.try_reserve(arms.len().checked_add(1).ok_or_else(unavailable)?).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    work.push((scrutinee.ordinal() as usize, owner));
                    for arm in arms {
                        match &arm.pattern {
                            yu_hir::shadow::LocalSourceCasePattern::Binder(binder) => {
                                self.intern(Value::CatchBinder(binder.id.clone()))?;
                            }
                            yu_hir::shadow::LocalSourceCasePattern::Tuple { elements, .. } | yu_hir::shadow::LocalSourceCasePattern::Variant { elements, .. } => {
                                for element in elements {
                                    if let yu_hir::shadow::LocalSourceCasePatternElement::Binder(binder) = element {
                                        self.intern(Value::CatchBinder(binder.id.clone()))?;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            yu_hir::shadow::LocalSourceCasePattern::Integer(_) | yu_hir::shadow::LocalSourceCasePattern::Wildcard => {}
                        }
                        work.push((arm.body.ordinal() as usize, owner));
                    }
                },
                LocalSourceForm::If { arms, else_branch } => {
                    let count = arms.len().checked_mul(2).and_then(|n| n.checked_add(usize::from(else_branch.is_some()))).ok_or_else(unavailable)?;
                    work.try_reserve(count).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    if let Some(else_branch) = else_branch {
                        work.push((else_branch.ordinal() as usize, owner));
                    }
                    for (condition, body) in arms {
                        work.push((condition.ordinal() as usize, owner));
                        work.push((body.ordinal() as usize, owner));
                    }
                },
                LocalSourceForm::Sequence { items } => {
                    work.try_reserve(items.len()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    for item in items { work.push((item.ordinal() as usize, owner)); }
                },
                LocalSourceForm::InterpolatedString { lowered: Some(inner), .. }
                | LocalSourceForm::FieldTail { receiver: inner, .. }
                | LocalSourceForm::GeneratedFormatSelection { receiver: inner, .. } => {
                    work.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    work.push((inner.ordinal() as usize, owner));
                },
                LocalSourceForm::InterpolatedString { parts, lowered: None, .. } => {
                    work.try_reserve(parts.len()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    for part in parts.iter().rev() { work.push((part.body.ordinal() as usize, owner)); }
                },
                LocalSourceForm::Record { fields } => {
                    work.try_reserve(fields.len()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    for field in fields.iter().rev() { work.push((field.value.ordinal() as usize, owner)); }
                },
                LocalSourceForm::Tuple { elements } | LocalSourceForm::Variant { payloads: elements, .. } => {
                    work.try_reserve(elements.len()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    for element in elements.iter().rev() {
                        work.push((element.ordinal() as usize, owner));
                    }
                },
                LocalSourceForm::Apply { callee, argument, .. } => {
                    work.try_reserve(2).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    work.push((callee.ordinal() as usize, owner)); work.push((argument.ordinal() as usize, owner));
                },
                LocalSourceForm::OperatorApplication { left, right, .. } => {
                    work.try_reserve(1 + usize::from(right.is_some())).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    work.push((left.ordinal() as usize, owner));
                    if let Some(right) = right { work.push((right.ordinal() as usize, owner)); }
                },
                LocalSourceForm::Group { inner } | LocalSourceForm::Ascription { inner, .. } => { work.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?; work.push((inner.ordinal() as usize, owner)); },
                LocalSourceForm::Lambda { parameter, body } => {
                    let defaults = parameter.record_pattern.iter().flatten().filter_map(|field| field.default.as_ref());
                    work.try_reserve(1 + defaults.clone().count()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    work.push((body.ordinal() as usize, owner));
                    for default in defaults { work.push((default.ordinal() as usize, owner)); }
                },
                LocalSourceForm::StatementBlock { statements, final_expression } => {
                    work.try_reserve(statements.len() + 1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    work.push((final_expression.ordinal() as usize, owner));
                    for statement in statements {
                        match statement {
                            yu_hir::LocalSourceStatement::Expression(expression) => work.push((expression.ordinal() as usize, owner)),
                            yu_hir::LocalSourceStatement::Binding(ordinal) => {
                                let binding = &source.bindings()[*ordinal as usize];
                                let local = self.intern(Value::Local(binding.id.clone()))?;
                                work.push((binding.initializer.ordinal() as usize, local));
                            }
                        }
                    }
                },
                LocalSourceForm::Block { bindings, final_expression } => {
                    work.try_reserve(bindings.len() + 1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                    work.push((final_expression.ordinal() as usize, owner));
                    for &binding in bindings {
                        let binding = &source.bindings()[binding as usize];
                        let local = self.intern(Value::Local(binding.id.clone()))?;
                        work.push((binding.initializer.ordinal() as usize, local));
                    }
                }, _ => {},
            }
        }
        // Fragment helpers are distinct from semantic Values. Every helper-only
        // path projects to the exact Name incidence, and every selected incidence
        // expands to such a path. Original-Value reachability and SCCs are unchanged.
        // Shared nested Apply/If fragments keep both work and storage linear.
        let mut fragments = Vec::new();
        fragments.try_reserve_exact(source.expressions().len()).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
        for expr in source.expressions() {
            if matches!(expr.form, LocalSourceForm::Name { .. } | LocalSourceForm::Apply { .. } | LocalSourceForm::If { .. } | LocalSourceForm::Sequence { .. } | LocalSourceForm::StatementBlock { .. } | LocalSourceForm::Tuple { .. } | LocalSourceForm::Record { .. } | LocalSourceForm::Variant { .. }) {
                self.edges.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                self.reverse.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                self.planning_levels.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
                fragments.push(Some(self.edges.len()));
                self.edges.push(Vec::new()); self.reverse.push(Vec::new());
                self.planning_levels.push(None);
            } else { fragments.push(None); }
        }
        for (index, expr) in source.expressions().iter().enumerate() {
            if let LocalSourceForm::Name { resolution, .. } = &expr.form {
                if let Some(value) = Self::resolved(resolution) {
                    let target = self.intern(value)?;
                    self.edge(fragments[index].unwrap(), target)?;
                }
            }
            if let LocalSourceForm::If { arms, else_branch } = &expr.form {
                let result = self.intern(Value::Expression(expr.occurrence.clone()))?;
                let fragment = fragments[index].unwrap();
                self.edge(result, fragment)?;
                for (condition, body) in arms {
                    for child in [condition, body] {
                        if let Some(child) = fragments[child.ordinal() as usize] { self.edge(fragment, child)?; }
                    }
                }
                if let Some(else_branch) = else_branch {
                    if let Some(child) = fragments[else_branch.ordinal() as usize] { self.edge(fragment, child)?; }
                }
            }
            if let LocalSourceForm::StatementBlock { statements, final_expression } = &expr.form {
                let result = self.intern(Value::Expression(expr.occurrence.clone()))?;
                let fragment = fragments[index].unwrap();
                self.edge(result, fragment)?;
                for item in statements.iter().filter_map(|statement| match statement {
                    yu_hir::LocalSourceStatement::Expression(expression) => Some(expression), _ => None,
                }).chain(std::iter::once(final_expression)) {
                    if let Some(child) = fragments[item.ordinal() as usize] { self.edge(fragment, child)?; }
                }
            }
            if let LocalSourceForm::Sequence { items } = &expr.form {
                let result = self.intern(Value::Expression(expr.occurrence.clone()))?;
                let fragment = fragments[index].unwrap();
                self.edge(result, fragment)?;
                for item in items {
                    if let Some(child) = fragments[item.ordinal() as usize] { self.edge(fragment, child)?; }
                }
            }
            if let LocalSourceForm::Record { fields } = &expr.form {
                let result = self.intern(Value::Expression(expr.occurrence.clone()))?;
                let fragment = fragments[index].unwrap();
                self.edge(result, fragment)?;
                for field in fields {
                    if let Some(child) = fragments[field.value.ordinal() as usize] {
                        self.edge(fragment, child)?;
                    }
                }
            }
            if let LocalSourceForm::Tuple { elements } | LocalSourceForm::Variant { payloads: elements, .. } = &expr.form {
                let result = self.intern(Value::Expression(expr.occurrence.clone()))?;
                let fragment = fragments[index].unwrap();
                self.edge(result, fragment)?;
                for element in elements {
                    if let Some(child) = fragments[element.ordinal() as usize] {
                        self.edge(fragment, child)?;
                    }
                }
            }
            let LocalSourceForm::Apply { callee, argument, .. } = &expr.form else { continue; };
            let result = self.intern(Value::Expression(expr.occurrence.clone()))?;
            let fragment = fragments[index].unwrap();
            self.edge(result, fragment)?;
            for child in [callee, argument] {
                if let Some(child) = fragments[child.ordinal() as usize] { self.edge(fragment, child)?; }
            }
            let receiver = self.intern(Self::expression_value(source, callee.ordinal() as usize))?;
            self.events.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
            self.checking.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
            self.checking_by_event.try_reserve(1).map_err(|_| unavailable())?;
            let event = self.events.len();
            let checking = ConstraintOccurrenceId::new(expr.occurrence.clone(), 0);
            self.checking.insert(checking.clone(), event);
            self.checking_by_event.push(checking);
            self.events.push(Association { origin: owners[index], receiver });
        }
        let scratch = checked_usize_sum([
            checked_capacity_bytes::<usize>(owners.capacity(), "Value owners"),
            checked_capacity_bytes::<(usize, usize)>(work.capacity(), "Value construction work"),
            checked_capacity_bytes::<Option<usize>>(fragments.capacity(), "Value fragments"),
        ], "Value construction scratch");
        self.construction_scratch_peak = self.construction_scratch_peak.max(scratch);
        Ok(fragments)
    }
    pub fn seal(&mut self) -> Result<(), SolveAvailabilityError> {
        if self.components.len() == self.edges.len() { return Ok(()); }
        let fail = || SolveAvailabilityError::IdentityExhausted;
        if self.planning_levels.len() != self.edges.len()
            || self.planning_levels.iter().any(Option::is_none) { return Err(fail()); }
        // This is the native premise of BirthLevelSCC's helper projection:
        // every edge into a helper preserves the actual source planning level.
        // Edges into semantic Values may cross levels and remain in raw Ref.
        for (source, targets) in self.edges.iter().enumerate() {
            for &target in targets {
                if !self.values_by_ordinal.contains_key(&target)
                    && self.planning_levels[source] != self.planning_levels[target] { return Err(fail()); }
            }
        }
        let mut seen = Vec::new(); seen.try_reserve_exact(self.edges.len()).map_err(|_| fail())?; seen.resize(self.edges.len(), false);
        let mut finish = Vec::new(); finish.try_reserve_exact(self.edges.len()).map_err(|_| fail())?;
        let mut stack = Vec::new(); stack.try_reserve_exact(self.edges.len()).map_err(|_| fail())?;
        for start in 0..self.edges.len() {
            if seen[start] { continue; } seen[start] = true; stack.push((start, 0));
            while let Some((node, next)) = stack.last_mut() {
                if *next == self.edges[*node].len() { finish.push(*node); stack.pop(); }
                else { let child = self.edges[*node][*next]; *next += 1;
                    if self.planning_levels[*node] == self.planning_levels[child] && !seen[child] {
                        seen[child] = true; stack.push((child, 0));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        self.seal_scratch_peak = checked_usize_sum([checked_capacity_bytes::<bool>(seen.capacity(), "SCC seen"), checked_capacity_bytes::<usize>(finish.capacity(), "SCC finish"), checked_capacity_bytes::<(usize, usize)>(stack.capacity(), "SCC stack")], "SCC scratch");
        self.components.try_reserve_exact(self.edges.len()).map_err(|_| fail())?;
        self.components.resize(self.edges.len(), usize::MAX);
        for start in finish.into_iter().rev() {
            if self.components[start] != usize::MAX { continue; }
            self.components[start] = start; stack.push((start, 0));
            while let Some((node, _)) = stack.pop() { for &child in &self.reverse[node] {
                if self.planning_levels[node] == self.planning_levels[child]
                    && self.components[child] == usize::MAX { self.components[child] = start; stack.push((child, 0)); }
            }}
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn qualifies(&self, event: usize) -> bool {
        self.events.get(event).is_some_and(|event| self.components.get(event.origin).zip(self.components.get(event.receiver)).is_some_and(|(a,b)| a == b))
    }
    pub fn bytes(&self) -> usize {
        checked_usize_sum([
            checked_capacity_bytes::<(DefinitionRootId, DefId)>(self.modules.capacity(), "Value modules"),
            checked_capacity_bytes::<(Value, usize)>(self.vertices.capacity(), "source Values"),
            checked_capacity_bytes::<(usize, Value)>(self.values_by_ordinal.capacity(), "source Value ordinal index"),
            checked_capacity_bytes::<Vec<usize>>(self.edges.capacity() + self.reverse.capacity(), "Value adjacency"),
            self.edge_bytes,
            self.identity_bytes,
            self.copied_module_payload_bytes,
            checked_capacity_bytes::<Association>(self.events.capacity(), "extrusion associations"),
            checked_capacity_bytes::<(ConstraintOccurrenceId, usize)>(self.checking.capacity(), "source associations"),
            checked_capacity_bytes::<ConstraintOccurrenceId>(self.checking_by_event.capacity(), "source checking event index"),
            checked_capacity_bytes::<usize>(self.components.capacity(), "Value SCCs"),
            checked_capacity_bytes::<Option<u32>>(self.planning_levels.capacity(), "Value birth and helper planning levels"),
        ], "source Value graph")
    }
}

pub type SourceText = str;

/// Source-level facts discovered in the syntax preamble.
#[derive(Clone, Eq)]
pub struct HeaderInfo {
    source: Arc<SourceText>,
    coverage: HeaderCoverage,
    imports: Arc<[HeaderImport]>,
    operators: Arc<[HeaderOperator]>,
}

impl std::fmt::Debug for HeaderInfo {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.debug_struct("HeaderInfo")
            .field("coverage", &self.coverage)
            .field("imports", &self.imports)
            .field("operators", &self.operators)
            .finish()
    }
}

impl PartialEq for HeaderInfo {
    fn eq(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {
        self.coverage == other.coverage
            && self.imports == other.imports
            && self.operators == other.operators
    }
}

impl HeaderInfo {
    pub fn coverage(&self) -> &HeaderCoverage {
        &self.coverage
    }

    pub fn imports(&self) -> &[HeaderImport] {
        &self.imports
    }

    pub fn operators(&self) -> &[HeaderOperator] {
        &self.operators
    }
}

/// The source prefix observed while discovering header facts.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]

fn is_exact_canonical_statement_intro(item: &Item) -> bool {
    matches!(
        item_word(item),
        Some("use" | "mod" | "struct" | "type" | "for" | "my" | "our" | "pub")
    )
}

pub(crate) fn next_use_item_lex(
    mut i: LexIn,
    item_origin: &mut usize,
    line_entry: &mut LineEntry,
    fence: Option<&FenceBoundary>,
) -> Item {
    let before = i.remainder().len();
    let CurrentItem {
        item,
        next_line_entry,
    } = current_item(
        i.rb(),
        *item_origin,
        *line_entry,
        fence,
        |lex, _, _, _, _| scan_use_payload(lex),
    )
    .expect("Use scanning is total");
    *item_origin += before - i.remainder().len();
    *line_entry = next_line_entry;
    item
}

fn next_use_item(
    mut i: SyntaxIn,
    item_origin: &mut usize,
    line_entry: &mut LineEntry,
    fence: Option<&FenceBoundary>,
) -> Item {
    i.token(|lex| Some(next_use_item_lex(lex, item_origin, line_entry, fence)))
        .unwrap()
}

===== R275-E005 | crates/yu-syntax/src/declaration/use_decl.rs:1577-1669 | source sha256 1f7df7e6101cc398056b610536c723271ed108aeecafe38eb09b8dc62cc45fc0 | excerpt sha256 9a48cec77cb04f5de2c57d3c7b5d2585f990504e63cc70e658a9c00dacaef122 | use-payload-and-lexical-scanners =====
fn scan_use_payload(mut i: LexIn) -> Option<AcceptedPayload> {
    let token = if let Some(version) = i.token(scan_version_token) {
        version
    } else if let Some(identifier) = i.token(scan_identifier) {
        identifier
    } else if let Some(arrow) = i.token(scan_arm_arrow) {
        arrow
    } else if let Some(separator) =
        i.token(|lex| scan_pair_token(lex, ':', ':', TokenKind::PathSeparator))
    {
        separator
    } else if let Some(punctuation) = i.token(scan_punctuation) {
        punctuation
    } else if let Some(slash) = i.token(|lex| scan_character_token(lex, '/', TokenKind::Operator)) {
        slash
    } else if let Some(operator) = i.token(scan_operator_token) {
        operator
    } else {
        i.token(scan_unknown)?
    };
    Some(AcceptedPayload {
        payload: CurrentPayload::Token(token),
        next_line_entry: LineEntry::InLine,
    })
}

fn scan_version_token(mut i: LexIn) -> Option<Token> {
    let (accepted, text) = i.rb().with_str(|mut version| {
        (version.next()? == 'v').then_some(())?;
        version
            .remainder()
            .chars()
            .next()
            .is_some_and(|character| character.is_ascii_digit())
            .then_some(())?;
        version.next()?;
        while version.remainder().chars().next().is_some_and(|character| {
            character.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(character, '.' | '-' | '+')
        }) {
            version.next()?;
        }
        Some(())
    });
    accepted?;
    Some(Token {
        kind: TokenKind::Identifier,
        text: text.into(),
    })
}

fn scan_pair_token(mut i: LexIn, first: char, second: char, kind: TokenKind) -> Option<Token> {
    let (accepted, text) = i.rb().with_str(|mut pair| {
        (pair.next()? == first).then_some(())?;
        (pair.next()? == second).then_some(())
    });
    accepted?;
    Some(Token {
        kind,
        text: text.into(),
    })
}

fn scan_character_token(mut i: LexIn, expected: char, kind: TokenKind) -> Option<Token> {
    let (accepted, text) = i
        .rb()
        .with_str(|mut one| (one.next()? == expected).then_some(()));
    accepted?;
    Some(Token {
        kind,
        text: text.into(),
    })
}

fn scan_operator_token(mut i: LexIn) -> Option<Token> {
    let (accepted, text) = i.rb().with_str(|mut spelling| {
        is_use_operator_character(spelling.next()?).then_some(())?;
        while spelling
            .remainder()
            .chars()
            .next()
            .is_some_and(is_use_operator_character)
        {
            spelling.next()?;
        }
        Some(())
    });
    accepted?;
    Some(Token {
        kind: TokenKind::Operator,
        text: text.into(),
    })
}
===== END R275-E005; omitted surrounding regions are NOT evidence or assumed no-ops =====
===== R275-E006 | crates/yu-syntax/src/declaration/use_decl.rs:1692-1735 | source sha256 1f7df7e6101cc398056b610536c723271ed108aeecafe38eb09b8dc62cc45fc0 | excerpt sha256 373440bfc141dc8127d1578edefa7049e2f21856f0d73fb5d1d5583224f7bcf9 | use-item-word-and-tree-starter-helpers =====
fn item_word(item: &Item) -> Option<&str> {
    (item.payload_view().token_kind() == Some(TokenKind::Identifier))
        .then(|| item.payload_view().spelling())
        .flatten()
}

fn exact_char(item: &Item, expected: char) -> bool {
    item.leading_view().is_grammar_empty()
        && item
            .payload_view()
            .spelling()
            .is_some_and(|text| text.len() == expected.len_utf8() && text.starts_with(expected))
}

fn inline_gap(item: &Item) -> bool {
    !item.leading_view().is_grammar_empty() && !item.leading_view().contains_line_break()
}

fn inline_keyword(item: &Item, expected: &str) -> bool {
    inline_gap(item) && item_word(item) == Some(expected)
}

fn inline_version(item: &Item) -> bool {
    inline_gap(item) && item_word(item).is_some_and(version_starter)
}

fn separator(item: &Item) -> Option<Separator> {
    if !item.leading_view().is_grammar_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    match item.payload_view().spelling() {
        Some("::") => Some(Separator::ColonColon),
        Some("/") => Some(Separator::Slash),
        _ => None,
    }
}

fn use_tree_starter(item: &Item) -> bool {
    exact_char(item, '{')
        || exact_char(item, '(')
        || item_word(item).is_some_and(|word| word == "mod" || use_identifier_spelling(word))
}

fn use_tree_starter_source(source: &str) -> bool {

===== R275-E007 | crates/yu-syntax/src/lexical/current_item.rs:1-250 | source sha256 41efcce2b737a0362484e854a8549bda5f7bf0d2b911499553f1e449c6782a84 | excerpt sha256 03bb7b2e9bcd6597858b0b3bf7046b3b56c24cd71c248cfd3f1dcb37fba64c21 | current-item-recognition-owner =====
//! One transient, fence-aware lexical current-Item constructor.
//!
//! This module owns only a live lexical transaction and one Item's physical
//! leading parts.  Grammar owners receive its completed Item immediately;
//! neither the line fact nor the fence capability is retained.

use reborrow_generic::short::Rb;

use crate::{
    cursor::LexIn,
    lexical::{
        item::{ForeignSplit, Item, Payload, PendingFragments, PhysicalLeadingTrivia},
        lexer::{FencedBlockComment, scan_block_comment_fenced, scan_trivia_part},
        yumark::{FenceBoundary, FenceLineDecision, judge_fence_line},
    },
};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq)]
pub(crate) enum LineEntry {
    PhysicalStart,
    InLine,
}

pub(crate) enum CurrentPayload {
    Token(crate::lexical::item::Token),
    Operator(crate::lexical::item::OperatorToken),
}

pub(crate) struct AcceptedPayload {
    pub(crate) payload: CurrentPayload,
    pub(crate) next_line_entry: LineEntry,
}

pub(crate) struct CurrentItem {
    pub(crate) item: Item,
    pub(crate) next_line_entry: LineEntry,
}

/// Constructs exactly one current Item in one lexical transaction.
///
/// `None` is an optional lexical non-match: input and recoverable state roll
/// back with the transaction, while local leading parts and split storage are
/// dropped.  A returned boundary is instead an accepted leading-only Item.
pub(crate) fn current_item<P>(
    mut i: LexIn,
    item_origin: usize,
    line_entry: LineEntry,
    fence: Option<&FenceBoundary>,
    payload: P,
) -> Option<CurrentItem>
where
    P: FnOnce(
        LexIn,
        bool,
        usize,
        Option<&FenceBoundary>,
        &mut Option<Vec<ForeignSplit>>,
    ) -> Option<AcceptedPayload>,
{
    i.token(|lex| scan_current_item(lex, item_origin, line_entry, fence, payload))
}

fn scan_current_item<P>(
    mut i: LexIn,
    item_origin: usize,
    line_entry: LineEntry,
    fence: Option<&FenceBoundary>,
    payload: P,
) -> Option<CurrentItem>
where
    P: FnOnce(
        LexIn,
        bool,
        usize,
        Option<&FenceBoundary>,
        &mut Option<Vec<ForeignSplit>>,
    ) -> Option<AcceptedPayload>,
{
    let source = i.remainder();
    let mut leading = PhysicalLeadingTrivia::default();
    let mut foreign = None;
    let mut has_ordinary_leading = false;

    if let Some(fence) = fence
        && line_entry == LineEntry::PhysicalStart
        && let Some(boundary) = classify_line(
            i.rb(),
            source,
            item_origin,
            fence,
            &mut leading,
            &mut foreign,
        )
    {
        return Some(boundary_item(leading, foreign, item_origin, boundary));
    }

    loop {
        if let Some(fence) = fence
            && i.remainder().starts_with("/*")
        {
            let part_origin = suffix_coordinate(source, item_origin, i.remainder());
            let comment = i.token(|comment| {
                scan_block_comment_fenced(comment, part_origin, fence, &mut foreign)
            })?;
            match comment {
                FencedBlockComment::Complete(comment) => {
                    has_ordinary_leading = true;
                    leading.push_ordinary(comment);
                    continue;
                }
                FencedBlockComment::Boundary { accepted, pending } => {
                    leading.push_ordinary(accepted);
                    return Some(boundary_item(leading, foreign, item_origin, pending));
                }
            }
        }

        let Some(trivia) = i.token(scan_trivia_part) else {
            break;
        };
        let starts_next_physical_line = trivia.has_line_feed();
        has_ordinary_leading = true;
        leading.push_ordinary(trivia);
        if starts_next_physical_line {
            if let Some(fence) = fence
                && let Some(boundary) = classify_line(
                    i.rb(),
                    source,
                    item_origin,
                    fence,
                    &mut leading,
                    &mut foreign,
                )
            {
                return Some(boundary_item(leading, foreign, item_origin, boundary));
            }
        }
    }

    if let Some(fence) = fence
        && i.remainder().is_empty()
    {
        let coordinate = suffix_coordinate(source, item_origin, i.remainder());
        let FenceLineDecision::Boundary(boundary) =
            judge_fence_line(i.remainder(), coordinate, fence)
        else {
            unreachable!("physical EOF is always a fence boundary");
        };
        return Some(boundary_item(leading, foreign, item_origin, boundary));
    }

    if fence.is_none() && i.remainder().is_empty() {
        return Some(CurrentItem {
            item: Item::plain(leading.into_ordinary(), Payload::Eof),
            next_line_entry: LineEntry::InLine,
        });
    }

    let payload_origin = suffix_coordinate(source, item_origin, i.remainder());
    let AcceptedPayload {
        payload,
        next_line_entry,
    } = payload(i, has_ordinary_leading, payload_origin, fence, &mut foreign)?;
    let payload = match payload {
        CurrentPayload::Token(token) => Payload::Token(token),
        CurrentPayload::Operator(operator) => Payload::Operator(operator),
    };
    let item = match fence {
        None => Item::plain(leading.into_ordinary(), payload),
        Some(_) => Item::finish(leading, payload, foreign, item_origin)
            .expect("one current Item owns ordered in-range foreign splits"),
    };
    Some(CurrentItem {
        item,
        next_line_entry,
    })
}

fn boundary_item(
    leading: PhysicalLeadingTrivia,
    foreign: Option<Vec<ForeignSplit>>,
    item_origin: usize,
    boundary: crate::lexical::item::PendingBoundary,
) -> CurrentItem {
    let next_line_entry = if matches!(
        boundary.kind(),
        crate::lexical::item::Boundary::EofAfterTrivia
    ) {
        LineEntry::InLine
    } else {
        LineEntry::PhysicalStart
    };
    CurrentItem {
        item: Item::finish(leading, Payload::Boundary(boundary), foreign, item_origin)
            .expect("a pending boundary retains only accepted current-Item parts"),
        next_line_entry,
    }
}

fn classify_line(
    mut i: LexIn,
    source: &str,
    item_origin: usize,
    fence: &FenceBoundary,
    leading: &mut PhysicalLeadingTrivia,
    foreign: &mut Option<Vec<ForeignSplit>>,
) -> Option<crate::lexical::item::PendingBoundary> {
    let coordinate = suffix_coordinate(source, item_origin, i.remainder());
    match judge_fence_line(i.remainder(), coordinate, fence) {
        FenceLineDecision::Boundary(boundary) => Some(boundary),
        FenceLineDecision::Body { prefix: None, .. } => None,
        FenceLineDecision::Body {
            prefix: Some(prefix),
            content,
        } => {
            let length = content
                .checked_sub(coordinate)
                .expect("accepted prefix stays on its physical line");
            let (_, text) = i.rb().with_str(|prefix| consume_bytes(prefix, length));
            PendingFragments::record(
                foreign,
                ForeignSplit::quote_prefix(prefix.facts.extent.start, length),
            )
            .expect("a judged quote prefix is one ordered in-range split");
            leading.push_quote_prefix(text.into());
            None
        }
    }
}

fn consume_bytes(mut i: LexIn, length: usize) -> Option<()> {
    let mut consumed = 0usize;
    while consumed < length {
        consumed = consumed.checked_add(i.next()?.len_utf8())?;
    }
    (consumed == length).then_some(())
}

fn suffix_coordinate(source: &str, item_origin: usize, suffix: &str) -> usize {
    let consumed = source
        .len()
        .checked_sub(suffix.len())
        .expect("a current Item cannot extend its entry suffix");
    assert_eq!(source.as_ptr().wrapping_add(consumed), suffix.as_ptr());
    item_origin
        .checked_add(consumed)
        .expect("a current-Item source coordinate must fit usize")
}
